### [1] [Agentic AI-enabled discovery across large-scale sleep physiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25175)
*Rahul Thapa,Umaer Hanif,Robin Guillard,Andreas Brink-Kjaer,Adrien Specht,Matteo Saibene,Magnus Ruud Kjaer,Harrison G. Zhang,Elisabeth Roxane M. Heremans,Eric C. Landsness,Emmanuel Mignot,James Zou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究开发了AI睡眠合作科学家系统，在约124,000份PSG记录和超过50TB原始信号数据上，通过五个案例研究揭示了睡眠生理与疾病风险、临床分型及睡眠调节机制的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠占据人类生命的三分之一，但其生理机制仍知之甚少。大规模多导睡眠图（PSG）数据集为研究睡眠及其与疾病的关联提供了新机遇，但从这些记录中提取洞见需要大量专家工作，且通用AI系统难以胜任。

Method: 开发了AI睡眠合作科学家（AI Sleep Co-Scientist）——一种专家引导环境，让人类科学家指导专业智能体进行假设开发、信号预处理和统计分析，并审查中间输出。每个报告结果都链接到生成该结果的可执行代码。研究涵盖四个队列约124,000份PSG记录，超过50TB原始信号数据。

Result: （1）睡眠期间网络级生理耦合减弱与帕金森病（HR 1.48）和阿尔茨海默病（HR 1.38）发病相关；（2）生理结构化的后期融合睡眠年龄模型优于非约束早期融合方法，其年龄残差与多器官系统的疾病发生相关；（3）觉醒动力学特征将共病失眠和睡眠呼吸暂停界定为偏向阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停的中间表型，特征为觉醒后清醒时间延长；（4）REM发作持续时间与之前的非REM睡眠关联更密切；（5）瞬态振荡分析识别出1型嗜睡症的快西格玛缺陷和额中央区过量θ活动。

Conclusion: 这些发现将睡眠与疾病风险、临床分类及睡眠自身调节联系起来，展示了智能体AI如何支持大规模、多模态科学发现。

Abstract: Sleep occupies roughly one-third of human life, yet many aspects of its physiology remain poorly understood. Large polysomnography (PSG) datasets offer new opportunities to study sleep and its links to disease, but extracting insight from these recordings requires substantial expert effort and remains difficult for general-purpose AI systems. We developed AI Sleep Co-Scientist, an expert-guided environment in which human scientists direct specialist agents for hypothesis development, signal preprocessing, and statistical analysis, reviewing intermediate outputs. Each reported result is linked to the executable code that produced it. Across four cohorts of approximately 124,000 PSG recordings and more than 50 TB of raw signals, we conducted five case studies spanning how sleep physiology relates to future disease, how it distinguishes clinical phenotypes, and how sleep is organized and regulated. Diminished network-level physiological coupling during sleep was associated with incident Parkinson's disease (HR 1.48) and Alzheimer's disease (HR 1.38). A physiologically structured late-fusion sleep-age model outperformed an unconstrained early-fusion approach, and its age residual was associated with incident disease across multiple organ systems. Arousal dynamics characterized comorbid insomnia and sleep apnoea as an intermediate phenotype skewed towards obstructive sleep apnoea, distinguished by prolonged post-arousal wakefulness. Rapid eye movement (REM) bout duration tracked preceding non-REM sleep more closely than intervening wakefulness. Transient-oscillation analysis identified a fast-sigma deficit and excess centrofrontal theta activity in narcolepsy type 1. Together, these findings connect sleep to disease risk, clinical classification, and its own regulation, and show how agentic AI can support large-scale, multimodal discovery.

</details>


### [2] [SafeFlow: Semantic Information-Flow Control for Blocking Malicious Propagation in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25255)
*Haowen Dai,Zonghao Ying,Wenfeng Li,Xiangfan Wu,Yisong Xiao,Tianyuan Zhang,Jiaye Lin,Lei Wei,Guangyuan Dong,Xitong Ling,Xixun Lin,Quanchen Zou,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体系统中的安全盲点：恶意意图可能通过任务分解规避检测。作者提出SafeFlow防御框架，将恶意跨智能体传播形式化为语义信息流问题，通过结构化语义污点传播和工作流级验证来检测风险，在四类基准测试上显著降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统通过任务分解和角色专业化提升能力，但这些机制引入了重要的安全盲点：有害目标可以被分解为局部合理的子任务，使得恶意意图能够规避任何单个智能体的检测。处理敏感信息或关键工具的系统可能将例行委托转化为未授权披露或不安全行动，这是一个日益增长的社会影响挑战。

Method: 提出SafeFlow防御框架，将恶意跨智能体传播形式化为语义信息流问题。SafeFlow为根请求附加结构化语义污点，通过动态协作图传播污点，并在执行不可逆操作前进行工作流级验证以重建全局风险上下文。

Result: 在四类基准测试（提示注入、基于越狱的不安全工具使用、风险代码执行、有害网络智能体行为）上，相比无防御基线和外部防御方法，SafeFlow显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了较高的良性任务完成率和高配对安全-有害成功率。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统仍缺乏在委托边界间保留风险语义的机制，这一缺陷可能将例行委托转化为影响人员和组织的隐私危害或不安全行动。SafeFlow在整个工作流中保持风险可见性，防止其演变为实际伤害。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems improve capability through task decomposition and role specialization, but these same mechanisms introduce an important safety blind spot: a harmful objective can be fragmented into locally plausible subtasks, allowing malicious intent to evade detection by any single agent. This is a growing social-impact challenge: systems handling sensitive information or consequential tools can turn routine delegation into unauthorized disclosure or unsafe action. We argue that this failure mode is better understood as a semantic information-flow problem than as a single-turn prompt classification task. To address this, we propose SafeFlow, a defense framework for multi-agent systems that formalizes malicious cross-agent propagation as a semantic information-flow problem. SafeFlow attaches structured semantic taints to root requests, propagates them through a dynamic collaboration graph, and performs workflow-level validation to reconstruct the global risk context before irreversible actions are committed. Evaluated on four benchmarks spanning prompt injection, jailbreak-based unsafe tool use, risky code execution, and harmful web-agent behavior, SafeFlow reduces attack success rates compared to undefended baselines and external defenses while retaining high benign task completion and a high paired safe--harm success rate. Our findings show that multi-agent systems still lack mechanisms for preserving risk semantics across delegation boundaries. This gap can turn routine delegation into privacy harms or unsafe actions that affect people and organizations. SafeFlow keeps this risk visible throughout the workflow, before it results in harm.

</details>


### [3] [CoRenew: A large language model agent-based policy simulation platform for multifamily residential redevelopment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25447)
*Yudi Zhang,Yuming Lin,Li Tian,Yu Wang,Jianghao Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了CoRenew平台，利用基于LLM的智能体模拟多利益相关者谈判，评估多家庭住宅重建政策组合的效果，并通过真实案例验证了其行为真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多家庭住宅重建中的集体行动困境仍是政策设计的核心挑战。利益相关者会根据谈判环境和他人的反应持续调整决策，政策干预的时机和对象会显著重塑集体结果。由于现有仿真模型依赖预定义的行为规则，难以事前评估这些适应性反应。

Method: 开发CoRenew开源平台，使用基于大语言模型(LLM)的智能体模拟多利益相关者谈判。平台整合开源地理和人口数据生成合成居民，在替代政策设置下模拟谈判动态，并跨竞争目标比较政策绩效。支持数值和语义政策输入，内置可视化和结果导出工具。

Result: 通过324位居民的调查回复和一个真实重建案例九个月观察到的谈判过程验证了平台的行为真实性。平台具有模块化和适应性架构，可用于评估不同制度和文化背景下的政策。

Conclusion: CoRenew平台为政策制定者提供了一个有效工具，能够在政策实施前评估多家庭住宅重建中的复杂谈判动态和政策效果，支持跨不同制度和文化背景的政策评估。

Abstract: The difficulty of collective action remains a central challenge in the design of policies for multifamily residential redevelopment. Stakeholders continually adjust their decisions in response to evolving negotiation contexts and the reactions of others, meaning that when a policy intervenes and which stakeholders it targets can substantially reshape collective outcomes. Assessing these adaptive responses ex ante remains difficult because existing simulation models often rely on predefined behavioral rules. Here, we present CoRenew, an open-source platform that uses LLM-based agents to simulate negotiations among multiple stakeholders and evaluate the effects of alternative policy combinations. Integrating open source geographic and demographic data, the platform can generate synthetic residents, simulate negotiation dynamics under alternative policy settings and compares policy performance across competing objectives. It supports both numerical and semantic policy inputs and includes built-in tools for visualization and result export. We validate its behavioral realism against survey responses from 324 residents and a nine-month observed negotiation process from a real redevelopment case. With its modular and adaptable architecture, CoRenew can be used to assess policies across different institutional and cultural contexts.

</details>


### [4] [ARCHER: Agentic Rule and Compliance Harness for Executable Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25566)
*Chiraag Singh Anand,Xue Wen Tan,Lionel Teo,Eric Tan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARCHER框架，通过多智能体协同从建筑法规自动生成可审计的验证代码，实现透明、可扩展的BIM合规性检查，在降低75%成本的同时达到接近前沿API的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑合规性检查需要针对大型BIM设计验证数千条规则，人工检查耗时费力且不可扩展。现有自动合规检查器(ACCs)存在三大局限：难以跨场景泛化（针对特定规则集开发）、多为专有系统（验证代码不公开，用户无法核实监管意图是否被准确捕获）、以及部署成本高昂。

Method: 提出ARCHER（Agentic Rule and Compliance Harness for Executable Regulations），一种测试驱动、确定性编排的多智能体程序综合框架。该框架能够从监管规范自动生成可审计的验证代码。研究设计了六种递进复杂度的智能体框架分类体系，在四种骨干模型上进行评估，覆盖从第三方API到本地开源模型的数据治理层级，并在真实合规场景数据集上验证。

Result: ARCHER的确定性多智能体编排在所有骨干模型上均取得最高准确率，相比朴素单次提示基线，平均联合准确率提升82%。成本-准确率分析显示，使用ARCHER框架的自托管开源模型能以四分之一的成本达到前沿API准确率的97.8%，使数据主权合规检查具有实用性。

Conclusion: ARCHER成功实现了透明、可适应且可扩展的合规性检查，证明了多智能体编排在程序综合任务中的有效性。该框架使数据主权合规检查成为可能，为建筑行业提供了可审计、可泛化的自动化合规验证解决方案。

Abstract: Verifying building compliance requires validating thousands of rules against large Building Information Modeling (BIM) designs, which is laborious, capital-intensive, and unscalable. Existing Automated Compliance Checkers (ACCs) are often difficult to generalize across different scenarios, as they are typically developed for highly specific rule sets and use cases. In addition, many ACCs are proprietary, meaning the underlying verification code is not released to end users, so users cannot verify whether their regulatory intent can be accurately captured. We introduce ARCHER (Agentic Rule and Compliance Harness for Executable Regulations), a test-driven, deterministically orchestrated multi-agent program-synthesis harness that generates auditable verification code from regulatory Codes of Practice, enabling transparent, adaptable, and scalable compliance checking. To characterize what makes agentic synthesis work, we evaluate a taxonomy of six harnesses of increasing agentic sophistication across four backbone models, spanning realistic data-governance tiers (from frontier third-party APIs to a fully on-premise open-weights model) on a novel dataset derived from real-world compliance scenarios. ARCHER's deterministic multi-agent orchestration achieves the highest accuracy for every backbone, improving mean union accuracy by 82% over a naive single-pass prompting baseline. Our cost-accuracy analysis further shows that using the ARCHER harness, a self-hosted open-weights model can reach 97.8% of frontier-API accuracy at a quarter of the cost, making data-sovereign compliance checking practical.

</details>


### [5] [CHILL-Harness: Counterfactual Harness Learning for Efficient Reasoning in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25825)
*Jiarun Fu,Lizhong Ding,Sida Chen,Honglei Xin,Chunhui Zhang,Pengqi Li,Qiuning Wei,Ye Yuan,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了CHILL-Harness框架，通过因果学习和反事实推理实现Agent Harness的自适应编排，在保持或提升任务成功率的同时显著降低token消耗和执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM Agent依赖Harness系统协调上下文、工具和执行控制，但现有Harness采用手工设计或全局固定策略，无法适应任务需求、执行环境和状态变化，导致不必要的计算开销甚至任务失败。

Method: 将自适应编排建模为因果学习问题：开发因果干预效应学习从置信加权执行证据中估计干预相对的工作流优势；引入优势实现因果编排自适应分配反事实推理；加入成功保持目标与优势边界授权约束确保可靠适应。

Result: 在信息检索、软件工程、终端交互等异构长时域任务上的实验表明，CHILL-Harness在保持或提升任务成功率的同时，显著降低了token消耗和执行时间。

Conclusion: 通过因果干预学习实现Harness层的自适应控制是可行的，能够在不牺牲任务性能的前提下提升推理和执行效率，为长时域Agent系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Agent harnesses have become the operational infrastructure of modern large language model agents, coordinating context, tools, verification, and execution control to translate latent model capability into reliable long-horizon behavior. However, reliable long-horizon behavior requires harness control to adapt to task demands, execution environments, and evolving execution states, whereas current harnesses predominantly rely on hand-crafted or globally fixed policies; this mismatch manifests as unnecessary computational overhead and, in adverse cases, reduced task success. To address this limitation, we formulate the task of enabling adaptive orchestration in harness systems as a causal learning problem and propose Counterfactual Harness Intervention Learning for Long-Horizon Agents (CHILL-Harness). CHILL-Harness intervenes at the orchestration layer to enable advantage-guided workflow adaptation, thereby improving reasoning and execution efficiency while preserving task performance. Specifically, we develop causal intervention effect learning as the effect-estimation component of CHILL-Harness to estimate intervention-relative workflow advantage from confidence-weighted execution evidence and identify advantageous workflow adaptations. We further introduce advantage-realizing causal orchestration as its realization component to adaptively allocate counterfactual reasoning and realize only workflow adjustments supported by sufficient expected advantage. Finally, we incorporate a success-preserving objective and advantage-margin authorization constraints into CHILL-Harness to promote reliable adaptation. Extensive experiments on heterogeneous long-horizon tasks spanning information seeking, software engineering, and terminal interaction show that CHILL-Harness consistently preserves or improves task success while substantially reducing token consumption and execution time.

</details>


### [6] [Towards a Systems Foundation for Agentic Cloud Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25883)
*Minghao Li,Ziqian Liu,Ziyu Mao,Daqian Ding,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Tianyin Xu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了CloudWeaver系统，为云管理智能体提供跨智能体和智能体与人协作的系统基础，通过局部视图和并发协调机制实现安全有效的自主云管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自主管理智能体快速发展，但缺乏一个系统基础来支持智能体之间、以及智能体与人类操作员之间的安全有效协作。现有系统无法有效处理并发操作冲突、资源访问控制和意图协调等关键问题。

Method: 提出CloudWeaver作为智能体管理基础架构：(1)通过云资源的局部视图界定各个智能体会话的上下文范围；(2)协调对共享云资源的并发管理操作。该系统跨现有云用户接口和未来原生智能体接口工作。

Result: CloudWeaver在存在意图冲突时提供强安全保证和可归因反馈，同时保持独立操作间的并发性。通过代表性Azure API工作负载验证了系统有效性。

Conclusion: 智能体云管理需要一个系统基础来确保安全和有效性，CloudWeaver为自主管理智能体提供了必要的协调机制和安全保障，填补了这一关键空白。

Abstract: Agentic cloud management is emerging as a practice to automate laborious operations, minimize toil, and improve responsiveness. Despite the rapid development of autonomous management agents, we argue that the fundamental missing piece is a systems foundation to enable safe, effective operations across agents and between agents and human operators. In this paper, we advocate for the need of such a systems foundation and share our efforts on developing CloudWeaver, an agentic management substrate that works across existing cloud-user interfaces and future agent-native interfaces. Specifically, we discuss how CloudWeaver (1) scopes the context of individual agent sessions with local views of cloud resources and (2) coordinates concurrent management operations on shared cloud resources. CloudWeaver offers strong safety guarantees and attributable feedback in the presence of conflicting intents, while preserving concurrency between independent operations. We validate CloudWeaver using a representative Azure API workload.

</details>

### [7] [Enhancing Error Detection Performance through Parallel CRC Computation on Multi-Core Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24849)
*Mohammad Javad Khani,Mahmood Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于POSIX线程的多核并行CRC框架，支持多种CRC变体，通过GF(2)上的组合机制保证并行计算的正确性，实验显示在大数据集上可获得约3-4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件CRC实现存在固有的串行依赖性，无法高效利用现代多核处理器，限制了性能提升。

Method: 提出基于POSIX线程的通用并行CRC框架，支持CRC-8/16/32/64/128等多种变体；采用GF(2)上的CRC组合机制替代简单的XOR聚合，通过多项式运算和矩阵移位操作确保并行计算与串行计算的等价性。

Result: 在多种工作负载和线程配置下评估，并行执行显著提升大数据集性能，在测试平台上获得约3-4倍加速，同时保持CRC计算的正确性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为通用多核系统提供了一个可移植、通用的软件框架，在保持正确性的前提下实现并行CRC加速，并与查找表、slicing-by-8、SIMD/向量化CRC及硬件辅助CRC等技术进行了对比分析。

Abstract: Cyclic Redundancy Check (CRC) remains one of the most widely used error-detection mechanisms in communication, storage, and embedded systems. However, conventional software CRC implementations suffer from inherent sequential dependencies that limit efficient utilization of modern multi-core processors. This paper presents a generalized software-based parallel CRC framework for multi-core architectures using POSIX threads. The proposed framework supports multiple CRC variants, including CRC-8, CRC-16, CRC-32, CRC-64, and CRC-128, within a unified implementation model. To preserve correctness during parallel execution, the framework employs a GF(2)-based CRC combination mechanism rather than naive XOR aggregation. The combine stage is formulated using polynomial arithmetic and matrix-based shifting operations over GF(2), ensuring equivalence between parallel and serial CRC computation. The proposed method was evaluated using multiple workload sizes and thread configurations. Experimental analysis includes execution time, throughput, latency, scalability behavior, and energy estimation under varying thread counts. Results indicate that parallel execution significantly improves performance for large datasets, achieving approximately 3-4x speedup on the evaluated platform while preserving exact CRC correctness. Comparative discussion with representative CRC optimization approaches, including lookup-table methods, slicing-by-8, SIMD/vectorized CRC, and hardware-assisted CRC techniques, is also provided to position the proposed framework within the broader CRC optimization landscape. Overall, the proposed approach provides a portable and generalized software framework for correctness-preserving parallel CRC acceleration on general-purpose multi-core systems.

</details>


### [8] [Spanergy: Energy-aware Distributed Tracing for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24902)
*César Perdigão Batista,Denis Conan,Sophie Chabridon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Spanergy，一种能耗感知的分布式追踪方法，将微服务功耗测量与追踪数据关联，把实测能耗归因到请求片段，实现了微服务能耗的可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算数据中心能耗持续增长，微服务作为云应用重要构建块缺乏能耗观测方案。现有分布式追踪技术仅诊断延迟和故障，无法暴露单个终端用户请求的能耗成本，限制了能耗感知的调试、问责和控制能力。

Method: 将每个微服务的功率测量与追踪数据（traces）关联，把实测能耗归因到请求片段（trace spans）；涵盖同步请求链和异步交互两种场景；设计严格的实验协议和统计分析方案来量化开销并验证守恒性和覆盖性。

Result: 启用OpenTelemetry追踪使总实验能耗相对于未插桩基线增加59.1%，Spanergy后处理额外增加基线能耗的15.2%。Spanergy的增量能耗成本小于启用追踪本身的能耗开销，具有轻量特性。此外，发现相当一部分请求能耗来自延迟关键路径之外的spans。

Conclusion: 能耗感知的分布式追踪在较低开销下可行，能为构建能效更高的微服务提供可操作的洞察。

Abstract: Cloud computing is gaining popularity by giving access to seemingly unlimited virtual resources. However, Cloud data centres are built with physical resources and their electricity consumption has been continuously growing over the past decades. Microservices are an important building block of Cloud applications, calling for new solutions to observe their energy consumption. Distributed tracing is widely deployed to diagnose latency and failures in microservice-based applications, yet it does not expose the energy cost of individual end-user requests. Such a gap limits energy-aware debugging, accountability, and control. This paper presents Spanergy, an energy-aware distributed tracing approach that correlates per-microservice power measurements with traces and that attributes measured energy consumption to request segments, i.e. trace spans. We showcase Spanergy with synchronous request chains and asynchronous interactions across microservices. We present a rigorous experimental protocol and statistical analysis plan to quantify overhead and to validate conservation and coverage properties on realistic configurations. Enabling OpenTelemetry tracing increased total experiment energy by 59.1% relative to the uninstrumented baseline, and Spanergy post-processing added 15.2% of the baseline energy. Hence, Spanergy's incremental energy cost is smaller than the energy overhead of enabling tracing itself, making the approach lightweight in practice. Spanergy also reveals that a non-negligible fraction of request energy comes from spans outside the latency-critical path. These results show that energy-aware tracing is feasible at modest overhead and provides actionable insights for energy-efficient microservices.

</details>


### [9] [QCOEM: Quantum Cloud Orchestration with Evolutionary Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25358)
*Tam N. Pham,Hoa T. Nguyen,Quan Le-Trung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出QCOEM框架，利用多目标进化算法优化异构量子云平台的任务调度，实现零重调度和约30%的保真度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子云平台编排器使用噪声不可知的启发式方法，忽略后端特定错误，导致执行保真度降低、负载不平衡和频繁重调度。由于量子计算后端的噪声特性、量子比特拓扑结构和队列随时间变化，需要动态编排工作负载。

Method: 提出QCOEM框架，采用NSGA-II和NSGA-III多目标进化算法联合优化平均完成时间、执行错误率和负载不平衡；使用增强成就标量化函数（AASF）作为偏好决策规则，从非凸Pareto前沿中选择符合用户优先级的单一可调度方案。

Result: 在异构量子云环境下的性能评估显示：实现零任务重调度；相比噪声不可知启发式方法，平均保真度提高约30%；调度开销保持在有限范围内。

Conclusion: QCOEM框架能够为量子云计算提供稳定、高保真度执行和轻量级资源管理，有效解决异构量子后端的动态调度问题。

Abstract: Quantum cloud platforms need to dynamically orchestrate workloads across heterogeneous quantum computation backends whose noise profiles, qubit topologies, and queues vary over time. Existing orchestrators use noise-agnostic heuristics that ignore backend-specific errors, causing reduced execution fidelity, load imbalance, and frequent rescheduling. To address these challenges, we propose QCOEM - a Quantum Cloud Orchestration framework that leverages Evolutionary algorithms for Multi-objective optimization of quantum task scheduling. We compare NSGA-II and NSGA-III for jointly minimizing mean completion time, execution error rate, and load imbalance. To select schedules from a non-convex Pareto front, we apply an Augmented Achievement Scalarization Function (AASF) as a preference-based decision rule that maps the Pareto set to a single dispatchable schedule aligned with user priorities. Our extensive performance evaluation in a heterogeneous quantum cloud environment shows zero task rescheduling and about 30% higher mean fidelity than noise-agnostic heuristics, while maintaining bounded scheduling overhead. The experiment results indicate that our QCOEM framework can deliver stable, high-fidelity execution and lightweight resource management for quantum cloud computing.

</details>


### [10] [CW-Ghost: Search-Free Granularity Selection for Helper-Thread Prefetching via Capacity Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25363)
*Ya Zhang,Tong Lei,Yao Chen,Yonggang Che,Chuanfu Xu,Haozhong Qiu,Yusong Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CW-Ghost通过容量窗口机制优化辅助线程预取的覆盖范围选择，在Intel和AMD平台上相比原始程序分别获得1.54x和1.33x的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助线程预取技术通过提前执行地址依赖链来隐藏不规则内存访问的延迟，但其有效性取决于辅助线程覆盖的未来迭代范围。固定覆盖范围无法适应不同工作负载和处理器，而穷举评估候选配置会产生大量配置开销。

Method: 提出CW-Ghost方法，使用单次离线分析运行来估计目标区域中每个目标迭代产生的平均需求缓存行填充量，结合缓存容量预算推导出容量窗口，用于确定每个预取块的迭代粒度。同时采用有界的块级同步来限制辅助线程领先主线程的块数。

Result: 在Intel和AMD CPU平台的14个工作负载实例上，CW-Ghost分别获得1.54x和1.33x的几何平均加速比。相比Ghost Threading分别提升15.8%和10.8%的性能，并在两个平台上均达到候选集中经验最优性能的99%以上。

Conclusion: 缓存容量约束能够有效指导辅助线程预取粒度的选择，证明了基于容量窗口的方法在优化不规则内存访问预取方面的有效性。

Abstract: Helper-thread prefetching hides the latency of irregular memory accesses by executing address dependency chains ahead of the main thread. However, its effectiveness depends on the range of future iterations covered by the helper thread. A fixed coverage range cannot consistently accommodate different workloads and processors, whereas exhaustively evaluating candidate configurations incurs substantial configuration cost. This paper presents CW-Ghost, which uses a single offline profiling run to estimate the average demand cache line fill volume generated per target iteration in a target region. CW-Ghost combines this estimate with a cache capacity budget to derive a Capacity Window, which determines the iteration granularity of each prefetch chunk. In addition, bounded chunk-level synchronization limits the number of chunks by which the helper thread may run ahead of the main thread. Across 14 workload instances evaluated on Intel and AMD CPU platforms, CW-Ghost achieves geometric mean speedups of 1.54x and 1.33x, respectively, over the original programs. Compared with Ghost Threading, it improves geometric mean performance by 15.8% and 10.8%, respectively, while achieving more than 99% of the empirically optimal performance within the candidate set on both platforms. These results demonstrate that cache capacity constraints can effectively guide the selection of granularity for helper-thread prefetching.

</details>


### [11] [WASP: A Configurable Framework for Portable Stateful Serverless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25493)
*Matteo Cenzato,Dario d'Abate,Arianna Dragoni,Giacomo Orsenigo,Luca Tosetti,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了WASP，一个可配置的有状态Serverless框架，通过解耦组件实现边缘-云连续体中对异构硬件和工作负载约束的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WebAssembly的无服务器平台与特定执行引擎紧密耦合，且主要针对无状态工作负载设计，这与边缘部署的异构性需求（需支持有状态应用及多样化硬件/工作负载约束）产生冲突。

Method: 放弃单体架构，采用严格解耦的可插拔组件设计，允许管理员在不修改应用代码的情况下更换WASM运行时和数据存储；引入可配置的生命周期和缓存策略以适应非功能性需求。

Result: 实验表明WASP引入的运行时开销可忽略不计；通过更换运行时、数据存储及策略，能呈现显著不同的内存占用和延迟特性，证实了其对异构约束的适应能力。

Conclusion: WASP成功将可配置的有状态Serverless执行引入边缘-云连续体，验证了通过解耦架构适配异构部署约束的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: WebAssembly (WASM) is emerging as a lightweight alternative to containers for Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) across the edge-cloud continuum. However, existing WASM-based serverless platforms are tightly coupled to specific execution engines and predominantly designed for stateless workloads. This clashes with the heterogeneity of edge deployments, which demand support for stateful applications under diverse hardware and workload constraints. We introduce WASP, a configurable framework that brings stateful serverless execution to the edge-cloud continuum. By abandoning monolithic architectures in favor of strictly decoupled, pluggable components, WASP lets system administrators swap the WASM runtime and the datastore to fit available resources and application requirements, without altering application code. Configurable lifecycle and caching policies further enable fine-tuning for diverse non-functional requirements. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that WASP introduces negligible runtime overhead and, by swapping runtimes, datastores, and policies, exposes radically different memory and latency profiles, confirming its adaptability to the heterogeneous constraints of the edge-cloud continuum.

</details>


### [12] [PowerScale: Energy-Efficient Geo-Distributed Model Training with Federated Datacenter Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25650)
*Talha Mehboob,Zhe Xu,Michael Zink,David Irwin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出PowerScale系统，通过分层聚合和同步-异步混合模式，在保持训练精度的同时将大规模地理分布式AI训练的能耗降低至多3.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI训练的功率需求日益超过单个数据中心容量，地理分布式训练成为实际需求。然而现有工作主要优化收敛时间，采用单层聚合方式，存在三大能耗效率问题：同步障碍导致快速节点空闲、大规模全WAN更新主导通信能耗、固定同步频率在训练后期仍支付相同通信成本。

Method: 提出PowerScale分层聚合系统：1）基于网络延迟和电力可用性将站点组织成区域集群；2）采用同步-异步混合模式，站点通过快速本地链路频繁同步到附近集群聚合器，集群聚合器异步推送预聚合更新到全局聚合器；3）自适应同步策略根据训练进度调整集群同步频率以降低通信能耗。

Result: 在基于Flower的仿真环境中对100站点规模进行评估。PowerScale与单层基线相比在收敛时间上持平或略有改善，同时将能耗降低至多3.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过利用广域网延迟层次结构的分层聚合系统，可以有效解决大规模地理分布式AI训练的能耗效率问题，为功率受限场景下的训练提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The power demands of large-scale AI training increasingly exceed the capacity of any single data center, making geo-distributed training across power-constrained sites a practical necessity. Prior work optimizes such training mainly for time-to-accuracy using single-tier aggregation, where every site exchanges model updates directly with a central aggregator over the WAN each synchronization round, without accounting for the energy required to reach convergence. Single-tier aggregation is fundamentally energy-inefficient because synchronization barriers force faster sites to idle, full WAN updates dominate communication energy at scale, and fixed synchronization frequency keeps paying the same communication cost even when updates shrink late in training.
  To address these inefficiencies, we present PowerScale, a hierarchical aggregation system that exploits the latency hierarchy of wide-area networks. PowerScale organizes sites into regional clusters and applies a Sync-Async synchronization modality: sites synchronize frequently with a nearby cluster aggregator over fast local links, while cluster aggregators push pre-aggregated updates asynchronously to a global aggregator over the WAN. PowerScale forms clusters based on both network proximity and power availability, and uses an adaptive synchronization policy that reduces communication energy by adjusting how often clusters synchronize to training progress. This structure shortens synchronization barriers and replaces per-site WAN transmissions with fewer, pre-aggregated transmissions at a lower frequency. We evaluate PowerScale at 100-site scale in a Flower-based simulation environment. PowerScale matches or slightly improves time-to-accuracy compared with single-tier baselines while reducing energy consumption by up to 3.9x.

</details>


### [13] [Hermes: Low Tail-Latency Via Prefix Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25916)
*Alejandro Ranchal-Pedrosa,Dakai Kang,Neil Giridharan,Dahlia Malkhi,Mohammad Sadoghi,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Hermes是一种基于前缀共识的拜占庭容错（BFT）协议，通过使过期视图也能达成共识来解决传统领导者协议中的超时权衡问题，从而显著改善了尾延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于领导者的BFT协议存在一个固有的超时权衡问题：保守的超时设置会导致崩溃的领导者造成长时间停滞，激进的超时设置则会浪费仅是缓慢的领导者的视图。无论哪种方式，过期视图都被浪费，这种权衡而不是理想情况决定了尾延迟。

Method: Hermes采用前缀共识（prefix consensus）机制，投票携带按前缀关系排序的值，仲裁只需要可比较性而非相等性。每个进程在视图开始时广播一个合理的提案，并在收到领导者提案或超时时投出单次投票（没有无效票）。及时的诚实领导者可在2δ内完成其完整提案的最终确认；否则，任何n-f张投票（无需匹配）都能最终确认最重的公共前缀。

Result: 协议在n=5f+1进程的部分同步模型下运行，及时的诚实领导者可在2δ内最终确认完整提案；在领导者失败或缓慢时，任何n-f张投票都能最终确认最重的公共前缀，使得过期视图也能达成共识。实例化为最终性工具时，复杂度为每视图O(n²m̂+λn³)比特和O(n²)消息。

Conclusion: Hermes成功解决了传统领导者BFT协议中超时设置的两难问题，通过使过期视图也能达成最终确认，显著改善了协议的尾延迟性能，证明了在部分同步模型下实现高效BFT共识的可行性。

Abstract: Leader-based BFT protocols finalize through their leaders: a view whose leader is crashed or slow finalizes nothing, and the timeout that ends it admits no good setting. A conservative timeout turns every crashed leader into a long stall; an aggressive one voids the views of leaders that are merely slow. Either way the expired view is wasted, and this trade-off, not the good case, governs tail latency.
  Hermes makes expired views finalize. Hermes is a two-round rotating-leader protocol for $n=5f+1$ processes under partial synchrony built on prefix consensus: votes carry values ordered by a prefix relation, and quorums require comparability rather than equality. Every process broadcasts a justified proposal at view start and casts a single vote, for the leader's proposal upon delivery or for a fallback proposal at the timeout; there are no nullify votes. A timely honest leader finalizes its full proposal in $2δ$. Otherwise, any $n-f$ votes, which need not match, finalize the heaviest common prefix. We instantiate Hermes as a finality gadget over an available chain and over Autobahn-style multi-lane dissemination, where parent-relative delta tipcuts with explicit skips keep independent proposals comparable and sender-indexed erasure-coded amplification keeps complexity at $O(n^2\hat{m}+λn^3)$ bits and $O(n^2)$ messages per view, where $\hat{m}$ is the number of concurrent dissemination lanes in Autobahn and $λ$ the security parameter.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [14] [Optimization of Collaborative Semantic Communication Network Performance with Channel and Content Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25011)
*Defeng Zhou,Dongyu Wei,Siyao Li,Mingzhe Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种支持重要性感知的语义通信框架，通过有限反馈优先传输重要图像区域，使用VDAC-DNC算法优化资源分配，性能提升最高达18.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信框架对所有图像区域采用同等重要性进行传输，无法满足实际应用中对图像不同内容的差异化优先级需求。

Method: 基站将图像分割为子图像并提取语义信息，用户根据偏好重建图像并协作决策反馈类型（CSI或内容偏好）。构建优化问题最小化语义加权MSE，提出VDAC-DNC算法：结合Actor-Critic与价值分解网络处理连续动作分布，并通过动态邻域构建方法在小规模离散邻域中搜索最优动作，避免遍历大规模离散动作空间。

Result: 所提VDAC-DNC方案相比标准多智能体QAC方法性能提升5.04%，相比无反馈传输方案提升18.55%。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了基于用户偏好和信道状态的差异化语义传输，VDAC-DNC算法有效提升了训练效率和系统性能，为语义通信的实用性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Existing semantic communication frameworks treat and transmit all image regions with equal importance, which is not practical for real-world applications which may prioritize different content in an image. To address this issue, we propose a novel semantic communication framework that enables a transmitter to use limited channel and content feedback to prioritize the transmission of important image regions. In particular, in the proposed framework, a base station (BS) divides each image into sub-images, extracts their semantic information, and transmits them to users according to their preferences. The users will reconstruct the image based on the received sub-images and cooperatively decide when to send channel state information (CSI) or content-preference feedback under dynamic channels and limited resources. We formulate an optimization problem to minimize the semantic-weighted mean square error between the original image and the regenerated image by optimizing sub-channel allocation, users' power allocation, and feedback selection. To address this problem, a value decomposition actor- critic (AC) with dynamic neighborhood construction (VDAC-DNC) scheme is proposed. The proposed method combines AC with value decomposition networks to allow the BS to approximate discrete actions by a continuous action distribution, thus reducing the output dimension and improving training efficiency. The introduced DNC method further improves training efficiency by constructing a small discrete neighboring action space to search for an action with the maximum Q value, thus avoiding traversing the large discrete action space. Simulation results show that the proposed VDAC-DNC scheme can improve the performance by up to 5.04% and 18.55% compared to the standard multi-agent QAC method and the proposed method without feedback transmission.

</details>


### [15] [Methods for Path Set Attribute Calculation in Network Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25103)
*Giovanni Fiaschi,Carlo Vitucci,Thomas Westerbäck,Daniel Sundmark,Thomas Nolte*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对路径集合割集计算的高效算法，并建立了向量化计算框架，通过基准测试验证了性能，证明了先前提出的路径集合属性模型在实践中的可行性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图论及其网络应用（如电信、交通）中，路径查找是核心问题。虽然单路径算法已成熟，但处理多路径集合的方法尚不完善。前作提出了基于结构属性定义路径集合属性的正式模型，本文旨在解决该模型的实际实现问题。

Method: 提出了一种优化的割集计算算法，用于处理路径集合的非平凡计算任务；引入了向量化计算框架，将属性计算表示为矩阵运算，从而支持数组导向语言的简洁实现。

Result: 通过不同复杂度的网络模拟进行了系统性基准测试，验证了割集计算算法的性能表现，执行时间具有可预测性且处于可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 两项贡献为前作提出的路径集合属性模型奠定了实践基础，证明该模型的实现不仅在技术上可行，而且具有可预测、可接受的执行效率。

Abstract: In graph theory and its applications to networking, such as telecommunications or transportation, path-finding is a central problem. While single-path algorithms are well established, methods for handling sets of multiple paths are less developed. A companion paper introduced a formal model for defining attributes over sets of paths based on their structural properties; this paper addresses that model's practical implementation. We present an optimized algorithm for computing cut sets of a path set, a nontrivial task that can be infeasible without efficient methods, and validate its performance via systematic benchmarks on network simulations of varying complexity. Additionally, we introduce a vectorized computational framework that expresses property calculations as matrix operations, enabling concise implementations in array-oriented languages. Together, these contributions establish practical foundations for the companion model, demonstrating that its implementation is both feasible and characterized by predictable, acceptable execution times.

</details>


### [16] [Robust Unsupervised Network Intrusion Detection via Federated Learning with Selective Aggregation under Anomalous Sample Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25439)
*Shohei Kamiguchi,Takayuki Nishio*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种鲁棒的联邦学习训练方法，通过利用联邦学习对少数数据的代表性不足特性来减弱异常数据的影响，并引入选择性聚合机制（使用EM算法）排除异常客户端更新，从而在训练数据被异常样本污染的情况下实现有效的物联网网络入侵检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督网络入侵检测系统通常假设训练数据完全干净，但在实际物联网环境中，直接从部署设备收集的数据往往包含异常样本。这种数据污染会导致检测性能下降，因此需要开发能够在被污染的未标记训练数据下有效工作的鲁棒无监督检测方法。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：（1）利用联邦学习倾向于低估少数数据的已知局限性，减弱来自少数被攻击客户端的异常数据影响；（2）引入选择性聚合机制，通过EM算法量化本地客户端模型与全局参考之间的"距离"，检测并排除模型更新显著偏离多数派的客户端组，防止异常更新损害全局模型。

Result: 在多个网络入侵检测数据集上的实验表明，该方法在训练数据被异常样本污染的环境下优于现有方法，且随着异常比例增加仍能保持检测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了无监督异常检测中训练数据被污染的问题，为物联网环境中的网络入侵检测提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案，具有在实际部署场景中的应用价值。

Abstract: Network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) have become essential for Internet of Things (IoT) environments, as malware targeting IoT devices continues to evolve in sophistication. Unsupervised learning approaches offer a promising direction by removing the dependency on labeled datasets. However, the common assumption that training data are entirely clean is often violated in practice, particularly when data samples are collected directly from deployed network devices, where anomalies are likely to be present in the training datasets. Such contamination degrades detection performance and highlights the need for robust unsupervised NIDS methods capable of operating effectively under contaminated unlabeled training data.
  To address this issue, we propose a robust training methodology for anomaly detection (AD) that remains effective even in the presence of unlabeled anomalies. Our method consists of two primary components. First, we exploit a known limitation of federated learning (FL), namely its tendency to underrepresent minority data. By leveraging this characteristic, we attenuate the influence of anomalous data originating from a small number of compromised clients. Second, we introduce a selective aggregation mechanism during model aggregation, which quantifies the "distance" between local client models and a global reference. Specifically, we employ the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm to detect and exclude client groups whose model updates significantly diverge from the majority. This selective aggregation ensures that anomalous updates do not compromise the global model.
  Experiments conducted on multiple NIDS datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in environments contaminated with anomalous data. Furthermore, the proposed method maintains its detection performance even as the proportion of anomalies increases.

</details>


### [17] [WALoMA: A Multitask Wireless Foundation Model via Adaptive Low-Rank Masked Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25763)
*Madi Makin,Asmaa Abdallah,Abdulkadir Celik,Ahmed M. Eltawil*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为WALoMA的多任务无线基础模型，通过自适应低秩掩码自编码器解决了6G无线物理层中专用模型和标注数据稀缺的问题，在五个下游任务上取得了87.80%的综合得分，远超基线模型LWM的59.90%，且仅需训练14.68%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线物理层的深度学习模型多为专用、任务特定的模型，难以泛化到不同任务，同时无线场景中标注数据稀缺，导致依赖大量标注数据的传统方法难以应用。因此，需要一种能够从无标注数据中学习、具备强泛化能力的统一多任务基础模型。

Method: 提出WALoMA框架，采用掩码自编码器（MAE）范式从无标注信道数据中学习，将信道状态信息（CSI）作为通用模态，通过自监督信道重构学习可迁移表征。架构上引入2D位置编码以保持天线与子载波间的空间-频率关系，并采用低秩适应（LoRA）实现参数高效的微调。

Result: 在五个下游任务上分别取得：LoS/NLoS分类96.47%、波束预测80.45%、信道插值85.78%、信道估计99.12%、信道图表化77.18%的性能。综合得分为87.80%，显著优于LWM基线的59.90%，且平均仅训练14.68%的参数，在极少标注数据条件下仍保持良好性能。

Conclusion: WALoMA成功构建了一个统一的无线物理层多任务基础模型，通过自监督学习显著降低了对标注数据的依赖，在多个任务上展现出优越的性能和参数效率，为6G无线系统的智能化提供了一个高效、通用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a multitask wireless foundation model via adaptive low-rank masked autoencoders (WALoMA), a unified multi-task foundation model for sixth-generation (6G) wireless physical layer architectures, to address the limitations of specialized, task-specific deep learning models and the practical challenge of scarce labeled wireless datasets. By leveraging concepts inspired by foundation models, the proposed framework adopts a masked autoencoder (MAE) paradigm to learn from unlabeled channel data, to significantly reduce reliance on extensive annotations. The model treats wireless channel state information (CSI) as a universal modality and learns transferable representations through self-supervised channel reconstruction. Key architectural novelties include the use of 2D positional encoding (PE) to explicitly preserve the spatial-frequency relationships between antennas and subcarriers, and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient fine-tuning. The framework's efficacy is demonstrated across five downstream tasks, achieving individual scores of 96.47\% for LoS/NLoS classification, 80.45\% for beam prediction, 85.78\% for channel interpolation, 99.12\% for channel estimation, and 77.18\% for channel charting. Consequently, numerical results show that the proposed model achieves a composite score of 87.80\%, significantly outperforming the 59.90\% achieved by the large wireless model (LWM) baseline while training an average of only 14.68\% of total parameters, and maintaining strong performance even under extremely limited labeled data conditions.

</details>


### [18] [The Model in the Middle: Toward AI-Native Real-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25792)
*Ziqian Liu,Minghao Li,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出将全双工全向模型视为反馈循环中的有状态计算中间件，而非人类端点的替代品，并倡导构建AI原生实时通信栈以实现跨阶段协同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统架构将网络传输、模型服务和用户播放视为独立阶段进行局部优化，无法满足全双工全向模型中多模态连续交互的需求。现有框架缺乏对跨越通信、计算和播放的联合控制问题的解决能力。

Method: 提出三个跨阶段协同机制：（1）网络感知的推理调度；（2）执行感知的传输优先级；（3）兼顾网络与模型变化性的播放控制。同时构建原型系统Conflux进行验证。

Result: 初步实验表明，在网络降级情况下，该方法显著改善了响应延迟和播放截止期遵守情况。

Conclusion: 呼吁构建AI原生实时通信栈，以统一协调网络传输、模型计算和用户播放三者之间的状态与行动，实现真正的AI驱动实时交互。

Abstract: Full-duplex omni models are transforming human--AI interaction from turn-based exchanges into continuous multimodal conversations in which speaking, listening, and reasoning unfold concurrently. Rather than viewing the model as a replacement for a human endpoint, we argue for a new perspective: the model is a stateful computational middlebox inside a human-centered feedback loop, with network transport, model serving, and user playback jointly shaping how the interaction evolves. This perspective breaks the traditional boundaries among stages designed around local objectives. Rather than optimizing them in isolation, an AI-native real-time stack should allow the state of each stage to shape the actions of the others. We explore three cross-stage coordination opportunities: network-aware inference scheduling, execution-aware transport prioritization, and playback control that accounts for both network and model variability. We are building Conflux to explore these ideas, and preliminary results show substantial improvements in response latency and playback deadline adherence under network degradation. More broadly, we call for an AI-native real-time communication stack that resolve the joint control problem spanning communication, computation, and playback.

</details>


### [19] [Revisiting-Aware In-Orbit Edge Computing for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25813)
*Zehua Sun,Tao Ni,Kaiyan Cui,Weitao Xu,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Stride框架，一种面向地球观测的在轨边缘计算方法，利用卫星重访特性和时间冗余，只传输感兴趣区域，有效缓解了由有限下行带宽导致的重访周期延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测卫星通常遵循固定的重访周期，但有限的卫星下行带宽导致只能传输部分图像，推迟了完整数据集的时效性，这种现象被称为重访周期延迟。为了解决这一传输瓶颈，需要利用在轨计算能力优化数据交付流程。

Method: 提出名为Stride的重访感知在轨边缘计算框架。该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 单时相和多时相云检测器以减轻云污染；(2) 从粗到细的参考图像选择器以校正轨道偏差；(3) 集成局部变化检测器以处理波段间复杂性和像素级扰动。通过利用轨道重访特性在轨获取历史参考图像，并利用重访图像中的时间冗余仅传输感兴趣区域。

Result: 在Flat-Sat测试平台和星座模拟器上的实验表明，Stride将重访图像交付（RID）得分提高了4.55倍，将连接延迟降低了5.02倍，并将制图覆盖率扩大了2.56倍，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Stride框架通过在轨边缘计算有效利用重访特性和时间冗余，显著提升了地球观测数据的交付效率，为解决卫星下行带宽受限条件下的数据传输瓶颈提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Typically, Earth observation satellites follow a rule of revisiting cycle to periodically pass over the same area of the Earth at regular intervals, which is jointly determined by their orbital properties (e.g., eccentricity, inclination) and instrument characteristics (e.g., off-nadir pointing and swath capabilities). However, we have observed delays in perceived revisiting cycles where limited satellite downlink bandwidth allows only partial images to be delivered, pushing back the timeliness of the full set of data, which we term as revisiting cycle delay.
  In this paper, we present a revisiting-aware in-orbit edge computing framework for Earth observation termed Stride. Stride leverages the unique orbital revisiting properties to afford historical reference revisiting images onboard, and exploits the inherent temporal redundancy in the revisiting imagery to transmit only the Regions of Interest (RoIs). Specifically, Stride comprises a mono- and multi-temporal cloud indicator to alleviate cloud contamination, a coarse-to-fine reference selector for orbit deviation correction, and an ensemble-local change detector to address inter-band complexities and pixel-level perturbations. Experiments on a Flat-Sat testbed and a constellation simulator demonstrate Stride improves the Revisiting Imagery Delivery (RID) score by up to 4.55X, decreases the connectivity latency by 5.02X, and enlarges the mapping coverage by 2.56X, yielding state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [20] [Performance Evaluation of RF-powered IoT in Rural Areas: The Wireless Power Digital Divide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25817)
*Hao Lin,Mustafa A. Kishk,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了农村地区无源IoT设备的下行链路性能，发现部署在区域内部的设备相比边缘设备可获得约两倍的能量收集概率和整体覆盖概率；在半径小于100m的农村区域内部署100个AP时，超过80%的RF供能IoT设备可得到支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合数字鸿沟是未来移动网络的目标之一，在农村地区构建IoT网络是可行的解决方案，但农村地区远离城市中心，基础设施薄弱，因此研究农村无线网络的下行链路性能具有重要意义。

Method: 将农村区域建模为远离城市中心的有限区域；BSs服从密度为2D-Gaussian的非齐次泊松点过程（PPP），有限区域内固定数量的APs服从二项点过程（BPP）；每个时隙分为充电子时隙和传输子时隙，IoT设备在充电子时隙收集来自BSs和APs的RF信号能量，在传输子时隙利用收集的能量接收下行信号；基于最小能量需求和SINR两个参数，推导整体覆盖概率（OCP）。

Result: 分析了偏远程度对能量收集性能的影响；当忽略BSs影响时，分析了IoT设备位置和AP数量对覆盖概率的影响；位于农村区域内部的IoT设备相比边缘设备可获得约两倍的ECP和OCP；在半径小于100m的农村区域，部署100个AP时可支持超过80%的RF供能IoT设备。

Conclusion: 该研究为农村地区RF供能IoT网络的部署提供了理论依据，表明合理部署AP数量和位置可显著提升无源IoT设备的下行链路性能，有助于实现弥合数字鸿沟的目标。

Abstract: Bridging the digital divide is one of the goals of mobile networks in the future, and further building IoT networks in rural areas is a feasible solution. This paper studies the downlink performance of rural wireless networks, where IoT devices we consider are battery-less and powered only by ambient radio-frequency (RF) signals. We model a rural area as a finite area that is far from the city center. The base stations (BSs) in the whole city and the access points (APs) in the finite network both act as sources of wireless RF signals harvested by IoT devices. We assume that BSs follow an inhomogeneous Poisson Point Process (PPP) with a 2D-Gaussian density, and a fixed number of APs are uniformly distributed inside the finite area following a Binomial Point Process (BPP). The IoT devices we consider can harvest energy and receive downlink signals in each time slot, which is divided into two parts: (1) a charging sub-slot, where the RF signals from BSs and APs are harvested by IoT devices, and (2) a transmission sub-slot, where each IoT device uses the harvested energy to receive and process downlink signals. We consider two main system requirements: minimum energy requirement and signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR). Using these two parameters, we investigate the overall coverage probability (OCP) related to them. We first study the effect of remoteness in rural areas on energy harvesting performance. Then we analyze the influence of IoT device's location and the number of APs on coverage probability when the effect of BSs can be ignored. This paper shows that the IoT devices located inside the rural area can obtain about twice the ECP and OCP of IoT devices located near the edge. For the average downlink performance in rural areas with radii less than 100 m, more than 80% of the RF-powered IoT devices can be supported when there are 100 APs deployed.

</details>


### [21] [C-RE-ACT: Causal RE-ACTing Agent for O-RAN Forensic Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25828)
*Pau Baguer,J. Xavier Salvat Lozano,Gines Garcia-Aviles,Xavier Costa-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出C-RE-ACT框架，通过因果图与LLM结合实现O-RAN网络性能退化攻击的自动化分类诊断，在物理测试平台上实现89%的根因定位准确率和84%的异常分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN架构的开放性和模块化特性扩大了攻击面，其中性能退化攻击是最主要的安全威胁类型。这类攻击导致的数据包丢失和延迟激增难以与配置错误、瞬态拥塞等运维事件区分，导致支持工程师在事件响应分类阶段面临关键的人为瓶颈，需要快速判断将事件工单路由至网络维护还是升级至安全运营。

Method: 提出C-RE-ACT框架：首先使用结构无关模型(SAM)构建O-RAN指标的加权有向无环图(WDAG)，然后通过图同构网络(GIN)将因果拓扑编码为连续软提示符，并与驱动ReAct智能体的大语言模型(LLM)的语言空间对齐，从而生成可操作的事件报告。

Result: 在物理O-RAN测试平台的140个性能退化实验中：因果排序在89%的实例中将正确根因隔离在前三个候选中；图软提示将LLM在因果拓扑查询上的准确率从0.22提升至0.72；自主智能体在延迟异常和丢包异常分类上分别达到83%和84%的准确率。

Conclusion: C-RE-ACT框架成功实现了O-RAN网络性能退化事件的自动化分类诊断，有效解决了人工分类瓶颈问题，为网络维护与安全运营之间的快速决策提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: The shift to O-RAN architectures marks a turning point in cellular security, where increased openness and modularity directly translate into a broader attack surface. Among the security threats cataloged by the O-RAN Alliance Working Group 11, performance-degradation attacks constitute the largest class. These attacks induce packet losses and latency spikes that are hard to distinguish from operational events such as misconfigurations, transient congestion, or software regressions. Consequently, upon an adverse incident detection, support engineers must rapidly determine whether to route the corresponding incident ticket to network maintenance or escalate it to security operations. This triage phase represents a critical human-in-the-loop bottleneck in the incident response lifecycle. To address this vulnerability, we introduce C-RE-ACT (Causal RE-ACTing agent), an automated agentic triage framework designed to generate actionable incident reports. C-RE-ACT starts constructing a Weighted Directed Acyclic Graph (WDAG) over O-RAN metrics using the Structural Agnostic Model (SAM). The resulting causal topology is encoded into a continuous soft token via a Graph Isomorphism Network (GIN) aligned with the language space of the Large Language Model (LLM) powering a ReAct agent. We evaluate C-RE-ACT on a physical, O-RAN-compliant testbed across 140 distinct performance-degradation experiments. Empirical results demonstrate the causal ranking isolates the correct root cause within the top three candidates in 89% of instances. Furthermore, graph soft-prompting improves LLM accuracy on causal-topology queries from 0.22 (text-only baseline) to 0.72. The autonomous agent achieves anomaly classification accuracies of 83% for delay anomalies and 84% for packet-loss anomalies.

</details>


### [22] [Untangling Co-Drift: Proactive Multi-Intent Failure Prediction and Root-Cause Disambiguation for Self-Driving Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25989)
*Md. Kamrul Hossain,Walid Aljoby*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出MILD框架，将自主网络的控制循环抽象为连续遥测、实时分析和程序执行三个宏观意图，通过教师增强的混合专家架构实现从被动漂移检测到主动故障预测的转变，能有效识别意图故障的根本原因并提供充足的修复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自驱动网络依赖紧密耦合的监控、分析和执行功能，但这些功能之间存在因果耦合关系，单一故障会在不同意图间传播并引发连锁异常。现有被动方法难以区分真正的根本原因意图和症状性受害者意图，且基于阈值交叉的检测机制无法预留足够时间进行主动修复。

Method: 提出MILD框架，将意图保障从被动漂移检测重构为主动故障预测。采用教师增强的混合专家架构，通过混合目标联合优化意图故障预测和根因归因。利用SHAP可解释性实现KPI级诊断，通过多时间尺度建模实现动态意图故障紧急度估计。

Result: 在三种递增真实度的环境（统计基准、微服务应用、SDN边缘云测试平台）中进行了广泛评估。MILD实现了高故障检测率、强修复提前量以及准确的意图级根因消除歧义能力。

Conclusion: MILD作为下一代自主网络闭环保障的实用使能器，成功实现了从被动到主动的意图保障范式转变，为自驱动网络的愿景提供了有效的技术支撑。

Abstract: The vision of self-driving networks that monitor, reason, and act upon themselves with minimal human intervention relies on tightly coupled monitoring, analytics, and actuation functions. In this work, we treat these functions as three operational macro-intents: continuous telemetry, real-time analytics, and programmatic actuation, and formalize the health of each function as an intent that the network must continuously satisfy. A critical, yet underexplored, challenge stems from the causal coupling among these intents, where a singular fault within one macro-intent propagates as a co-drift and subsequently triggers cascading, symptomatic anomalies across the remaining intents. This ambiguity makes it exceedingly difficult for existing, reactive approaches to distinguish the true root-cause intent from symptomatic victim intents, and their reliance on threshold-crossing detection leaves insufficient time for proactive remediation. We introduce MILD, a novel framework that reformulates intent assurance from reactive drift detection to proactive failure prediction. Grounded in our three-macro-intent formulation of the self-driving control loop, MILD employs a teacher-augmented Mixture-of-Experts architecture with a hybrid objective that jointly optimizes intent failure prediction and root-cause attribution. MILD enables KPI-level diagnostics via SHAP explainability and dynamic intent failure urgency estimation via multi-horizon modeling. Our extensive evaluation of MILD across three environments of increasing realism, from a controlled statistical benchmark, to a microservices application, to an SDN-based edge-to-cloud testbed, demonstrates that MILD achieves high failure detection rates, strong remediation lead times, and accurate intent-level root-cause disambiguation. This positions MILD as a practical enabler of closed-loop assurance in next-generation autonomous networks.

</details>


### [23] [MAC-Gyver: Open, Programmable, Scheduling for AI-RAN 6G Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26012)
*Maxime Elkael,Reshma Prasad,Tamerlan Aghayev,Salvatore D'Oro,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAC-Gyver，一个开源框架，用于在OpenAirInterface调度器中直接开发和评估调度应用。该框架支持真实无线环境和模拟环境中的AI调度算法验证，通过两个用例展示了其在降低延迟和频率选择性调度方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的MAC调度器大多仅在仿真环境中评估，难以在生产环境中部署和验证。生产调度器难以修改，且真实的压力测试需要大量无线硬件资源，这在大多数实验室中难以实现。因此需要一个能够在真实协议栈中直接执行AI调度算法的框架。

Method: 提出MAC-Gyver开源框架，直接在OpenAirInterface调度器内部执行调度应用。该框架通过类型化接口暴露调度器的观测和控制，同时保留底层协议和实时执行路径。支持相同应用在真实无线环境和mac-emu仿真器上运行，后者是在单主机上以实时时隙节奏执行未修改的OpenAirInterface Layer 2栈的PHY-less仿真器，支持多达90个用户和3GPP兼容信道模型。

Result: 通过两个用例验证框架的灵活性：1）主动上行调度器通过预测数据包到达，将中位往返延迟降低约一半；2）频率选择性上行调度器从每个PRB探测观测中选择连续子带，在移动性和功率受限操作点下与离线调度上限进行评估对比。

Conclusion: MAC-Gyver成功地将生产级协议栈转化为AI实验平台，支持AI调度算法的实现、受控评估和空中验证，填补了AI调度器从仿真到生产部署之间的鸿沟，为蜂窝网络AI集成提供了可行的开发验证路径。

Abstract: Cellular networks are integrating Artificial Intelli- gence (AI) into radio access network control. The MAC scheduler is a promising target because it allocates a limited resource, spectrum, at every slot, under competing latency, throughput, and reliability requirements. However, most learning-based sched- ulers are evaluated only in simulation. Production schedulers are difficult to modify, and realistic stress tests require more radio hardware than most laboratories can provide. We present MAC-Gyver, an open-source framework for developing and evaluating scheduling applications that execute directly inside the OpenAirInterface scheduler. It exposes scheduler observations and controls through typed interfaces while preserving the underlying protocol and real-time execution paths. The same applications run over the air and in mac-emu, a PHY-less emulator that executes the unmodified OpenAirInterface Layer 2 stack for up to 90 users on one host at real-time slot pace, with a 3GPP-compliant channel model. To showcase the flexibility of MAC-Gyver, we evaluate two use cases. A proactive uplink scheduler predicts packet arrivals and roughly halves median round-trip latency. A frequency-selective uplink scheduler selects contiguous sub-bands from per-PRB sounding observations and is evaluated across mobility and power-limited operating points against an offline scheduling ceiling. Together, they show how the same production stack can be an AI playground that supports implementation, controlled evaluation, and over-the-air validation through complementary scheduling use cases.

</details>
