SKIP combines question-guided visual token pruning, region-conditional sparse retrieval, bipartite sparse cross-attention, and speculative knowledge verification with an adaptive budget controller that allocates compute proportional to predicted question difficulty. We derive an information-bottleneck bound showing that the optimal visual sparsity rate scales as $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ under realistic question-image mutual-information assumptions, with retained accuracy guarantees. Across five KI-MMQA benchmarks (OK-VQA, A-OKVQA, InfoSeek, Encyclopedic-VQA, and ViQuAE), SKIP matches or exceeds the accuracy of strong dense baselines while using $3.4$--$6.8\times$ fewer FLOPs and $2.7\times$ less end-to-end latency. Code available at: https://pmlrbd.github.io/skip/

</details>


### [92] [The Disruptive Impact of Large Language Models on Capture the Flag Competitions and the Path Toward Fair Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25425)
*Michael Macaulay,Harmony Bouabid,Guo Gen Ang,Sasha Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法分析了LLM对CTF竞赛的影响，发现AI已能可靠自动化简单和中等难度的密码学、Web和二进制利用挑战，并提出了四组件保障框架（分层竞赛、抗LLM题目设计、调查性遥测和社区行为准则）来应对AI带来的公平性和学习价值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型能够以最少的人类输入解决越来越多的CTF挑战，引发了关于竞赛公平性、排名有效性以及参与竞赛是否仍能提供值得付出努力的技能学习效果的紧迫问题，需要研究LLM对现代CTF竞赛的实际影响。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：综合已发表基准测试（包括政府评估）、三个挑战类别的现场竞赛案例研究、对社区讨论AI使用的公共频道进行结构化观察，以及对经验丰富的选手和组织者进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 绘制了各挑战类别的人机能力边界图，发现密码学、Web和二进制利用中的简单和中等难度挑战现在可以被可靠地自动化，而较窄的子类别仍能抵抗AI；发现社区对是否允许AI的分歧源于一个未声明的先验问题：竞赛的目的是什么。

Conclusion: 提出了四组件保障框架（分层竞赛组别、抗LLM挑战设计、调查性遥测和社区行为准则草案），以及将保障措施组合与竞赛既定目的联系起来的决策工具；该论证不仅适用于CTF，还适用于网络安全中任何将演示结果作为基础能力证据的场景。

Abstract: Capture the Flag (CTF) competitions are among cybersecurity's most effective training grounds, developing practical skill across cryptography, web exploitation, and binary exploitation. Large language models (LLMs) can now solve a growing share of challenges with minimal human input, raising urgent questions about fairness, the validity of rankings, and whether participation still delivers the learning that justifies the effort. This paper reports a mixed-methods study of LLM impact on modern CTFs, combining a synthesis of published benchmarks, including a recent government evaluation, case studies of live competition across three challenge categories, structured observation of the public channels where the community debates AI use, and semi-structured interviews with experienced players and organisers. We map the current human-machine capability boundary by category, showing that easy and intermediate challenges in cryptography, web, and binary exploitation are now reliably automated while narrower sub-categories continue to resist. We find that community disagreement about whether AI should be permitted is downstream of an undeclared prior question: what a competition is for. Against this backdrop we contribute a four-component safeguard framework, combining tiered competition divisions, LLM-resistant challenge design, telemetry used investigatively, and a draft community code of conduct, together with a decision tool that ties the combination of safeguards to a competition's declared purpose. The argument reaches beyond CTFs to any setting in cybersecurity where a demonstrated result is taken as evidence of an underlying ability.

</details>


### [93] [Toward an Organizational Science of Multi-Agent LLM Systems: Decoupling Who, How, and Which Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25446)
*Huan Chen,Xiang Song,Jian Jin,Pan Ren,Liang-Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IMACS框架，将多智能体系统中的组织结构、协调机制和协作协议三个关注点解耦为可独立配置的层，并引入自适应组织路由机制实现了动态协议选择，证明组织设计需针对不同模型重新验证或学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体框架常将组织结构（团队成员构成）、协调机制（成员对齐方式）和协作协议（工作融合算法）三个逻辑上独立的关注点混在一起，导致系统难以灵活配置和优化，缺乏对这些要素的系统性研究。

Method: 提出IMACS（智能多智能体协作系统）框架，将三个关注点分离为正交的、可独立替换的层级：1) 将经典组织理论（Belbin角色、Mintzberg协调、RACI问责制）转化为可执行的配置；2) 统一封装六种已发表的协作算法；3) 引入自适应组织路由（基于上下文赌博机的元协议），根据任务动态选择协议并在线学习。通过控制实验分离各因素影响。

Result: 自适应组织路由在显式质量-成本权衡下优于所有固定协议，可在真实基准和LLM评判奖励下在线训练。消融实验揭示：问责制安排仅在协议将交付物路由经过问责代理时影响结果；最优安排在不同模型系列间会翻转，说明组织设计不能硬编码，必须为每个模型绑定重新验证或学习。

Conclusion: 通过正交分层设计实现了组织因素与协作算法的独立研究，证明组织设计需要模型特定优化而非通用配置，为多智能体系统提供了灵活可扩展的框架基础，并展示了元学习协议选择的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent frameworks built on large language models (LLMs) routinely entangle three logically distinct concerns: who is on the team (organization), how members align (coordination), and which algorithm fuses their work (collaboration protocol). IMACS (Intelligent Multi-Agent Collaboration System) separates the three into orthogonal, independently swappable layers. Classic organizational theory (Belbin roles, Mintzberg coordination, RACI accountability) becomes executable, validated configuration, and the framework places six published collaboration algorithms behind a common interface while exposing roles, coordination, and accountability as independently configurable factors. We use this separation to conduct controlled comparisons in which organizational assignments vary while the collaboration protocol is held fixed. It also turns protocol choice into a variable that can be learned: Adaptive Org Routing, a contextual-bandit meta-protocol, selects a protocol per task under an explicit quality-cost tradeoff, outperforms every fixed protocol in a controlled study, and trains online on real benchmark and LLM-judge rewards. The ablations expose a mechanism. Accountability placement changes outcomes exactly when the protocol routes the deliverable through the accountable agent, and the winning placement flips across model families, so organizational design cannot be hard-coded; it must be revalidated, or learned, for each model binding.

</details>


### [94] [TRWH: A Text-Driven Random Walk Heterogeneous GNN for Semantic-Aware Sparse Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25471)
*He Ma,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TRWH框架，通过策略性随机游走增强将LLM生成的文本特征与异构图结构融合，在稀疏推荐场景下显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络(GNNs)擅长建模结构信号，大语言模型(LLMs)擅长捕捉语义信号，但如何融合二者的互补优势仍具挑战性，特别是在需要保持语义精度的稀疏场景中。

Method: 提出TRWH框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 嵌入创建模块，结合Word2Vec和LLM生成用户与物品表示；(2) 异构图神经网络(HeteroGNN)，在多关系边上传播信息；(3) 基于随机游走的路径构建，通过二阶用户-用户和物品-物品连接丰富稀疏图。

Result: 在Amazon-2023 Fashion数据集上，RMSE降低80.0%，MAE降低52.6%；在Beauty数据集上分别提升25.7%和10.8%。同时发现随机游走虽然能改善传统嵌入，但可能稀释LLM学习到的细粒度表示。

Conclusion: TRWH框架成功融合了LLM文本特征与异构图结构，在稀疏推荐场景下超越现有方法。研究强调了自适应集成策略的重要性，因为简单的随机游走可能损害LLM的语义表达能力。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) have each advanced recommendation systems by modeling structural and semantic signals, respectively. However, integrating their complementary strengths remains challenging, particularly in sparse settings where maintaining semantic precision is critical. We propose TRWH (Text-driven Random Walk Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network), a novel framework that fuses LLM-generated textual profiles with heterogeneous graph structures through strategic random walk augmentation. TRWH consists of three core components: (1) Embedding Creation, which produces user and item representations using both Word2Vec and LLM-based profiling; (2) a Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (HeteroGNN) that propagates information across multi-relational edges; and (3) Random Walk-based Path Construction, which enriches sparse graphs with second-order user-user and item-item links. Experiments on the Amazon-2023 Fashion (2M users, 825K items) and Beauty (631K users, 112K items) datasets demonstrate that TRWH achieves substantial performance gains over state-of-the-art methods, including 80.0% RMSE and 52.6% MAE reductions on Fashion, and 25.7% and 10.8% improvements on Beauty. Notably, while random walks improve performance with traditional embeddings, they can dilute the nuanced representations learned by LLMs, underscoring the importance of adaptive integration strategies.

</details>


### [95] [Balancing multiscale similarity and cartographic constraints: A similarity-driven optimization framework for line generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25474)
*Pengbo Li,Haowen Yan,Xiaomin Lu,Binbin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个相似性驱动的制图综合自适应控制框架，通过构建约束多尺度相似性优化问题，将相似性评估、制图约束与参数优化统一在一个目标函数中，实现了相似性保持与制图抽象之间的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化制图综合方法通常将空间相似性评估、制图约束和参数优化视为独立过程，缺乏跨尺度的自适应性和可解释性控制，限制了多尺度地图表示中信息保持与可读性之间的平衡。

Method: 将制图综合建模为约束多尺度相似性优化问题，提出相似性驱动框架。框架将多尺度空间相似性作为优化目标量化表示一致性，同时引入制图约束（可读性、平滑度、几何有效性）进行约束，构建统一目标函数以自动识别不同综合算法的尺度依赖参数配置。

Result: 使用多种线简化算法、目标尺度和相似性度量（几何、结构、学习型度量）进行实验，结果表明该框架在相似性保持与制图抽象之间取得了有效平衡。结合相似性优化与制图约束的参数控制比单独依赖相似性评估更一致且更具可解释性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了连接相似性评估、约束建模和算法控制的统一优化视角，为自适应和自动化制图综合提供了理论基础和技术支撑，推动了多尺度地图表示的发展。

Abstract: Cartographic generalization is essential for generating multiscale map representations by balancing information preservation and cartographic readability. However, automated generalization remains challenging because existing approaches often treat spatial similarity evaluation, cartographic constraints, and parameter optimization as separate processes, limiting adaptive and interpretable control across scales. This study formulates cartographic generalization as a constrained multiscale similarity optimization problem and proposes a similarity-driven framework for adaptive generalization control. The framework integrates multiscale spatial similarity as an optimization objective to quantify representation consistency between original and generalized data, while incorporating cartographic constraints to regulate readability, smoothness, and geometric validity. A unified objective function is optimized to automatically identify scale-dependent parameter configurations for different generalization algorithms. Experiments using multiple line simplification algorithms, target scales, and similarity measures, including geometric, structural, and learning-based metrics, demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an effective balance between similarity preservation and cartographic abstraction. The results further show that combining similarity optimization with cartographic constraints provides more consistent and interpretable parameter control than relying on similarity evaluation alone. This study provides a unified optimization perspective that connects similarity assessment, constraint modeling, and algorithm control, contributing to adaptive and automated cartographic generalization.

</details>


### [96] [Finding Optimal Cost-Bounded Plan Reductions: Refined Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25484)
*Martha Del Toro,Raquel Fuentetaja,Angel García-Olaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从预计算计划中提取满足成本约束且效用最大的有效子计划，证明该问题的判定版本是NP完全的，并提出两种精确求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，预计算的计划可能因新增预算约束而变得不可行，但必须保持原计划动作及其顺序不变，因此需要从原计划中提取满足成本约束的子计划。

Method: 为每个目标分配效用值，通过移除支持低效用目标的动作来削减计划，同时保持可执行性和原动作顺序；提出两种精确方法：基于超额订阅规划（OSP）和整数线性规划（ILP）的求解方法。

Result: 证明了该问题的判定版本是NP完全问题；提出的ILP方法通过精炼模型显著减小了模型规模并提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 该研究为在预算约束下从预计算计划中提取最大效用子计划提供了理论基础和高效求解方法，扩展了ICAPS 2026的前期工作。

Abstract: In some real applications a plan may later become unfeasible due to newly imposed budget constraints, yet, at the same time, using only the original actions of the plan and their order is mandatory. In this paper, we study the problem of extracting, from a precomputed plan, a valid subplan that maximizes utility while respecting a cost bound. Each goal is given a utility value and the plan is reduced by removing actions that support low-utility goals, while preserving both executability and the original action order. We show the decision variant is NP-complete and propose two exact methods to solve it: one via oversubscription planning (OSP) and another via Integer Linear Programming (ILP). This paper extends our previous work published at ICAPS 2026 (Del Toro, Fuentetaja, and García-Olaya 2026b). While the core framework remains as introduced there, we further introduce a refined ILP formulation that significantly decreases the model size and improves computational efficiency.

</details>


### [97] [PatientAgentBench: A Benchmark Framework for Evaluating Patient-Facing Health AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25485)
*Korosh Vatanparvar,Ashutosh Joshi,Maria Xenochristou,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Daniel Lopez-Martinez,Anchal Nema,Ramya Ganesan,Will Kimbrough,Alex Woody,Yadunandana Rao,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PatientAgentBench，首个面向患者的医疗智能体基准测试框架，通过模拟对话评估医疗AI的临床安全性和工作流程准确性。测试发现现有模型存在显著的诊断分诊和安全漏洞，最强模型整体得分仅4.25/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准测试主要关注医学知识问答或面向临床医生的任务，缺乏对面向患者的智能体系统的评估。随着医疗AI向具有推理和行动能力的智能体系统演进，需要评估其在初级护理场景中可能出现的诊断错误和医疗安全风险。

Method: 构建PatientAgentBench基准框架：将基础模型封装为具有医疗工具沙箱的智能体，与模拟患者进行对话；使用LLM-as-a-Jury通过100多个临床专家制定的维度无关标准，从六个维度评估对话质量；通过执业临床医生标注验证评分一致性（79-93%相邻一致性）；在1200个场景中对来自四个模型系列的10个模型进行测试。

Result: 分诊质量是区分度最高的维度：最弱模型通过率32%，最强88%，智能体常在无临床筛查情况下处理行政请求；临床安全和工作流程准确性呈现相同模式：最弱模型频繁伪造未执行动作，前沿模型仅1-3%案例失败（未验证工具输出、紧急情况遗漏危机资源）；能力更强的模型缩小但未消除差距，最强模型整体仅得4.25/5分。

Conclusion: 静态基准测试已不足以评估获得自主权的医疗智能体系统，只有通过持续的、使用工具的对话并在真实患者记录场景下测试才能发现潜在安全故障。发布该框架作为可重复、经临床验证的评估标准，推动领域填补这一差距。

Abstract: Health AI is evolving from answering questions to agentic systems that converse with patients, reason about health records, and act on their behalf. Primary care guards against diagnostic errors and unsafe care; agents assisting in this domain warrant evaluation against the same risks. Current benchmarks focus on medical knowledge, assessed through isolated question-answering or clinician-facing tasks. PatientAgentBench benchmarks patient-facing agentic healthcare; it evaluates a foundation model, wrapped in an agent with a sandbox of healthcare tools, conversing with a simulated patient. Each conversation is scored by an LLM-as-a-Jury across six dimensions via over a hundred conversation-agnostic, clinician-grounded criteria. To validate alignment, licensed clinicians annotated shared conversations, yielding 79-93% adjacent agreement between jury and expert raters, on par with or exceeding clinician inter-rater agreement. We benchmarked 10 models across four families on the same 1,200 scenarios and found clinical gaps. Triage quality is the most discriminating dimension: pass rates rise from 32% for the weakest models to 88% for the strongest, with agents often acting on administrative requests without clinical screening. Clinical safety and workflow accuracy follow the same pattern: the weakest models fail often, fabricating unexecuted actions, while frontier models fail on only 1-3% of cases, from unverified tool outputs and omitted crisis resources in an emergency. More capable models narrow these gaps but do not close them; the strongest scores only 4.25 of 5 overall. These failures surface only in sustained, tool-using conversations against realistic patient records, confirming that static benchmarks are insufficient as healthcare agentic systems gain autonomy. We release the framework as a reproducible, clinician-validated evaluation standard to help the field close this gap.

</details>


### [98] [CoTinyVLA: Chain-of-Thought Distillation for a Sub-Billion-Parameter Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25487)
*Minhyeok Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Chanhoe Gu,Seongrok Kim,Sanghyuk Roy Choi,Donghwan Hwang,Donghun Ryu,Seokhyun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoTinyVLA，一个0.9B参数的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过结构化监督而非扩大模型规模，在LIBERO-Plus基准上超越所有7B基线模型，同时在嵌入式机器人内存预算内运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型依赖3B到7B参数的大型骨干网络，其内存需求超出了嵌入式机器人的预算限制，亟需开发轻量级但高性能的替代方案。

Method: 提出三个组件：(1) 双视角时序输入，每步16帧历史帧并带有文本相机和时间标记；(2) 层次化思维链(CoT)蒸馏，从35B教师模型蒸馏出episode级Plan和chunk级Think；(3) 改写增强，将40个基础命令扩展为800个变体。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus基准上，CoTinyVLA在四个测试套件上分别达到90.8%、87.3%、86.6%和80.7%，超越最强7B基线4.7、2.8、15.9和3.0个百分点。在机器人初始状态维度上达到73.6%（最强基线仅39.9%）。闭环推理峰值GPU内存仅2.25 GiB。

Conclusion: 结构化监督使0.9B参数模型能够超越所有更大规模的基线模型，证明了通过精心设计的监督策略而非单纯扩大模型规模可以有效提升机器人操作系统的鲁棒性，同时满足嵌入式硬件的资源约束。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models translate natural-language commands into robot action sequences, but leading systems on the LIBERO-Plus robustness benchmark use three- to seven-billion-parameter backbones whose memory demands can exceed embedded robotic budgets. We present CoTinyVLA, a 0.9B-parameter action model on a Qwen3.5-0.8B backbone that obtains that robustness by structuring supervision instead of enlarging the model. Three components target different axes of the problem: dual-view temporal input of 16 history frames per step with textual camera and time markers; hierarchical chain-of-thought (CoT) distillation from a 35B teacher into an episode-level Plan and a chunk-level Think span over task phase, gripper state and next subaction; and paraphrase augmentation expanding 40 base commands into 800 variants. On LIBERO-Plus, spanning 10,030 perturbed tasks across seven perturbation dimensions, CoTinyVLA reaches 90.8% on Spatial, 87.3% on Object, 86.6% on Goal and 80.7% on Long, leading the strongest 7B baseline on all four suites by 4.7, 2.8, 15.9 and 3.0 points, with every margin interval excluding zero. The gains concentrate on the hardest axes of the benchmark: across the eleven published baselines none exceeds 53.2% on Robot Initial States in any suite, whereas CoTinyVLA reaches 73.6% on Goal against 39.9% for the strongest baseline. Ablations show the three components to be separable by perturbation axis, and at a matched image budget how frames are divided between the two cameras and across time accounts for 8.6 points on its own. Closed-loop inference peaks at 2.25 GiB of allocated GPU memory, and paired interventions show the episode Plan to be load-bearing: replacing it with an empty or contradictory span costs 40 to 45 points of success. Structured supervision thus lets a 0.9B backbone exceed all of them. Code: https://github.com/BrainJellyPie/CoTinyVLA

</details>


### [99] [Are the High-weight Neurons the Important Ones in Image Classification Neural Networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25529)
*Qitao Chen,Dongfu Yin,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现神经元权重与其重要性之间并非简单的线性关系：高权重神经元中存在大量非关键神经元，低权重神经元也可能具有重要影响，挑战了传统权重-重要性等价假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对神经元权重-重要性关系的清晰理解。尽管神经元在剪枝、后门防御和可解释性方面发挥关键作用，但高权重神经元是否等同于重要神经元这一问题尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 提出三种实验方法评估神经元重要性：(1)量化高权重神经元与影响精度神经元之间的重叠度；(2)分析高权重神经元扰动对模型精度的影响；(3)测试高权重神经元消融后重训练的精度恢复情况。在CIFAR-10和Mini-ImageNet数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验揭示关键发现：(1)前10%高权重神经元与重要神经元的重叠度仅约25%；(2)前10%高权重神经元扰动导致45-80%精度下降，但其中三分之一影响微弱；(3)移除前10%高权重神经元后精度比基线低10-20%且无法恢复，移除前0.1%可接近完全恢复；(4)部分低权重神经元扰动导致10-17%精度下降，与中等高权重神经元相当。

Conclusion: 证实并非所有高权重神经元都重要，其重要性呈非线性特征，低权重神经元同样具有显著贡献。该发现挑战了权重-重要性等价假设，为神经元角色理解提供新视角，支持加密优先保护关键高权重神经元、剪枝移除非关键神经元等应用场景。

Abstract: As neural network models for image classification advance, neurons play critical roles in pruning, backdoor defense, and interpretability. Yet existing work lacks clarity on the weight-importance relationship. We address this with a neuron importance assessment method using three experiments: quantifying overlap between high-weight and accuracy-impacting neurons, analyzing high-weight neuron perturbation effects, and testing post-retraining accuracy after high-weight neuron ablation. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and Mini-ImageNet reveal key patterns. Overlap analysis shows top 10\% high-weight neurons overlap with important ones by only about 25\% at maximum, dropping further in subsequent intervals. Perturbation tests find top 10\% high-weight neurons cause 45-80\% accuracy degradation under certain operations compared to 3-7\% for random perturbations, but a third of them show minimal impact. Ablation-retraining results show removing top 10\% high-weight neurons leaves accuracy 10-20\% below baseline with no recovery, while ablating top 0.1\% allows near-full recovery. Notably, some low-weight intervals show 10-17\% degradation when perturbed, comparable to mid-range high-weight neurons. These results confirm not all high-weight neurons are important: their importance is nonlinear. Low-weight neurons also contribute significantly. This challenges weight-importance equivalence, offering refined neuron role insights. It supports applications like encryption prioritizing critical high-weight neurons and pruning removing non-critical ones, advancing neural network analysis.

</details>


### [100] [Entangled by Design: Spurious Intra-Variable Signal Routing in Tabular In-Context Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25532)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,Giorgos Papanastasiou,Bernhard Kainz,Sotirios Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了上下文学习(ICL)在表格数据中存在虚假路由问题，即模型倾向于利用虚假信号而非因果信号进行预测，并提出了有效的轻量级缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际医疗场景中，模型可能在训练时依赖与医院设备相关的虚假信号而非患者的真实健康信号，导致在新环境中部署时出现静默失败。本文旨在形式化并解决上下文学习中的虚假路由问题。

Method: 提出了虚假路由在组合表示中的理论框架，证明在线性ICL和TabPFN模型中路由问题的不可避免性；推导出封闭形式的CSR表征；设计了两种轻量级缓解策略：环境分层上下文构建和S-swap增强。

Result: 线性ICL和TabPFN的CSR预测相关性分别达到r=0.997和r=0.979；更大的上下文会使虚假路由恶化1.74倍；S-swap方法能将线性ICL的虚假路由降低74%，TabPFN降低98.8%，同时TabPFN的因果敏感度提升8.4倍。

Conclusion: 虚假路由是ICL固有的问题，即使增加上下文也无法解决，但通过简单的环境感知增强方法可以有效缓解，使模型重新路由到因果信号上。

Abstract: Consider a model trained at a single hospital to predict patient recovery, where the measured feature $X$ bundles the patient's true health signal ($C$) with a systematic artefact from that hospital's equipment ($S$). Within that hospital, the artefact correlates with outcomes through unmeasured confounders such as patient demographics; an in-context learner rationally routes predictions through $S$, not $C$, and fails silently when deployed at a new hospital with different equipment. We formalise this as \emph{spurious routing in composite representations}: when a feature $X = [C;\,αS;\,η]$ encodes a causal signal $C$ and a spurious signal $S$ in distinct subspaces, the ICL cannot determine which drives predictions. We prove that under ridge ICL, a linear in-context learner, this routing is unavoidable regardless of context size; TabPFN, a state-of-the-art pretrained tabular ICL model, shows qualitatively consistent behaviour empirically. We derive a closed-form characterisation, $\mathrm{CSR} \propto ρ_S/ρ_C$, confirmed at $r = 0.997$ for linear ICL and $r = 0.979$ for TabPFN. Contrary to intuition, larger context sharpens commitment to the dominant in-context signal, amplifying spurious routing by up to $1.74\times$; in the high-spurious corner, more expressive models show greater vulnerability empirically ($+2.22$ CSR gap at high entanglement). We introduce two lightweight mitigations: environment-stratified context construction and S-swap augmentation, that require only weak environment labels and no knowledge of the causal partition. S-swap reduces spurious routing by $74\%$ for linear ICL and $98.8\%$ for TabPFN, with TabPFN's causal sensitivity increasing $8.4\times$ simultaneously: the model does not become agnostic, it reroutes through the causal signal.

</details>


### [101] [From Training to Deployment: Post-Hoc Causal Feature Identification via Sensitivity Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25546)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,Giorgos Papanastasiou,Bernhard Kainz,Sotirios Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为归一化灵敏度比（NSR）的方法，用于在模型训练完成后，识别模型是依赖因果特征还是伪相关特征。在结构化偏移场景下，NSR可以准确区分特征类型，无需访问训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要访问训练过程才能判断模型依赖特征的性质，但实际应用中常需要对已训练模型进行事后分析。在多站点临床数据或多批次基因组数据等场景中，环境差异主要表现为伪相关特征均值的偏移，而因果机制保持稳定，需要一种后验、模型无关的诊断方法。

Method: 提出归一化灵敏度比（NSR）指标，基于结构化偏移假设：因果特征在各环境中诱导恒定的模型灵敏度，而伪相关特征追踪环境偏移。NSR定义为各环境灵敏度变异系数的平方。采用线性结构因果模型框架，在K≥3个非退化环境下实现特征类型的精确识别。

Result: 理论证明：在线性SCM下实现精确识别，给出三种失效情况的完整刻画（弱偏移、退化几何、代理衰减）；有限样本收敛率为O_p(n^{-1})（零假设）和O_p(n^{-1/2})（备择假设）。实验验证：合成数据AUROC=1.000，五种模型家族Kendall τ≥0.529，自行车共享数据Precision@7=0.75。

Conclusion: NSR提供了一种有效的后验模型诊断工具，可在不修改已训练模型的情况下识别因果与伪相关特征，为实践者提供了评估结构化偏移假设是否成立的定量标准，在多环境数据场景中具有良好的理论和实证表现。

Abstract: Given a model that is already trained, which features does it rely on causally versus spuriously? Existing methods require access to the training procedure and cannot answer this post-hoc. We introduce the \textbf{Normalised Sensitivity Ratio~(NSR)}, a post-hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic for this question under a structured-shift regime: environments differ primarily in the mean of spurious features while the causal mechanism and causal marginals remain stable, as in multi-site clinical data or multi-batch genomics. Within this regime, causal features induce constant model sensitivity across environments while spurious features track shift. NSR formalises this as the squared coefficient of variation of per-environment sensitivity. Under a linear structural causal model (SCM) with $K\ge3$ non-degenerate environments, NSR achieves exact identification (Theorem~1). We fully characterise failure: weak shifts ($O(\varepsilon^4)$ collapse), degenerate geometry, and proxy attenuation ($O((1-α)^4)$), giving practitioners quantitative criteria for assessing whether the regime holds. Finite-sample rates are $O_p(n^{-1})$ under the null and $O_p(n^{-1/2})$ under the alternative. Experiments confirm all theoretical predictions on synthetic data (area under the ROC curve [AUROC] $= 1.000$ under conditions satisfying the regime), show consistent rankings across five model families (Kendall $τ\ge0.529$), and recover six of eight causal features on bike-sharing data (Precision@7 $= 0.75$) without modifying any trained model.

</details>


### [102] [Distilling Temporal Search and Reasoning: Evolving LLMs for Future Prediction via Harness-Assisted Efficient Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25554)
*Wanxu Cai,Zhengyu Chen,Huaisheng Zhu,Wei Wang,Jingang Wang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间截断框架来解决事件预测中的时间泄露问题，通过强制时间截止来提高数据采样效率，并证明了基于该框架蒸馏的模型在预测任务上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA方法依赖外部代理框架，移除框架后预测能力消失；虽然TIR方法已实现事实检索的内部化，但事件预测还需要对历史趋势和动态变化进行时间搜索与推理。核心障碍在于数据：历史查询会导致时间泄露，使预测退化为检索；现有工作要么冻结信息收集，要么依赖拒绝采样或未解决的新查询，导致大量数据被丢弃，降低了合成效率。

Method: 提出时间截断框架（time-truncation harness），在每一轮强制执行时间截止，支持历史事件的TIR式采样，减少时间泄露和对拒绝采样或未解决查询的依赖，提高采样效率。构建大规模语料库和基于过程的评估指标。

Result: 该框架自然地诱导了更广泛的时间搜索广度，提高了高质量数据的比例，进一步提升了效率并减少了对复杂规则的依赖。蒸馏实验显示，在框架干预数据上训练的学生模型达到了最佳性能。

Conclusion: 证明了框架辅助的模型进化能够将高质量的时间搜索与推理数据转化为学生模型的参数化进步，实现了将外部框架能力内化为模型自身预测能力的目标。

Abstract: Future event prediction carries broad social impact yet remains challenging. SOTA approaches augment LLMs with external agent frameworks whose predictive capability vanishes once the harness is removed. While recent Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) internalizes deep search for multi-hop retrieval of facts, forecasting further demands temporal search and reasoning over historical trends and dynamic shifts. The key obstacle is data: historical queries induce temporal leakage that degrades forecasting into retrieval. Prior works either freeze information gathering with static observations, or rely on rejection sampling or unresolved fresh queries that discard vast amounts of data, degrading synthesis efficiency. We propose a time-truncation harness that enforces a temporal cut-off at every turn, enabling TIR-style sampling from historical events, reducing temporal leakage and reliance of rejection sampling or unsolved queries, increasing the sampling efficiency. We further build a large-scale corpus and a process-based metric and show that our harness naturally induces a broader temporal breadth of search and raises the proportion of high-quality data, further increasing the efficiency and reducing the reliance on complex rubrics. Distillation experiments show that students trained on harness-intervened data achieve the best performance, demonstrating harness-assisted model evolving that turns higher quality temporal search and reasoning data into a parametric advancement of the students.

</details>


### [103] [Agent Skills Matter: Inferring Proprietary Skills from Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25560)
*Jianing Geng,Ruiqi He,Zekun Fei,Biao Yi,Ruijie Wang,Zheli Liu,Xia Hu,Xuansheng Wu,Qingkai Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了智能体技能存在"技能泄露"安全漏洞，提出了SigLeak框架，能够仅通过观察良性查询的执行轨迹来重构专有技能，在多种场景下证明了私有技能知识可被泄露的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体技能市场的兴起，技能提供者为保护商业利益将高价值技能保持私有。然而，作者发现虽然技能实现被隐藏，但其行为效应仍可通过执行轨迹被观察，形成行为侧信道。这一发现引发了对技能隐私安全的担忧，促使研究技能泄露问题。

Method: 提出SigLeak黑盒框架，利用智能体行为中重复出现的技能签名。该方法包含三个核心步骤：1）构建多样化、决策丰富的诊断任务；2）对比启用技能与禁用技能的匹配轨迹，识别差异模式；3）迭代优化重构技能。该方法无需参考答案或成功标签，仅依赖良性查询轨迹。

Result: 在五个场景、三个模型家族和三个智能体框架上进行了广泛评估。SigLeak在几乎所有设置下都优于或匹配三个基线方法。平均而言，相比禁用技能的基准，成功率提升6.88个百分点。在提出的SkillSim指标（衡量粗粒度和细粒度语义相似性）上达到最高水平。

Conclusion: 研究证明了良性执行轨迹可以暴露专有的过程性知识，揭示了技能市场中存在的安全隐患。这一发现对技能提供者和平台开发者都具有警示意义，需要考虑行为侧信道的保护机制。代码已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Agent skills package reusable procedures that improve downstream performance. Their lightweight, portable form enables marketplace monetization and private deployment behind cloud-hosted agent interfaces, giving providers incentives to keep high-value skills proprietary. Yet hiding the artifacts does not conceal their behavioral effects, which remain observable in execution trajectories and form a behavioral side channel. We define this exposure as Skill Leakage: reconstructing proprietary skills from trajectories elicited by benign queries, without reference answers or success labels. We introduce SigLeak, a black-box framework that exploits recurring skill signatures in agent behavior. It constructs diverse, decision-rich diagnostic tasks, contrasts matched skill-enabled and skill-disabled trajectories, and iteratively refines a reconstructed skill from the isolated patterns. Across five scenarios, three model families, and three agent frameworks, SigLeak outperforms or matches three baselines in nearly every setting. It raises the success rate by 6.88 percentage points over the skill-disabled reference on average and achieves the highest overall SkillSim, our metric for coarse- and fine-grained semantic similarity. These results show that benign execution trajectories can expose proprietary procedural knowledge. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SigLeak-D1DB.

</details>


### [104] [Matrix-Free Photoacoustic Image Reconstruction via Sensor-Token Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25576)
*Mary John,Shibili Said,Imad Barhumi,Sherzod Turaev,Mohamed Yahia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Sensor Attention Network (SAN)，一种基于Transformer的光声断层成像重建架构，通过将每个传感器的完整时间序列视为token，绕过系统矩阵实现实时重建，在各项指标上显著优于传统迭代方法和学习化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光声断层成像(PAT)从稀疏视角传感器测量中恢复初始压力分布是一个不适定逆问题。现有迭代压缩感知求解器和展开深度网络在推断时都依赖于系统矩阵，导致实时临床重建计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的Sensor Attention Network (SAN)架构，将每个传感器的完整时间序列视为token，直接将原始测量映射到重建图像，无需在推断时调用系统矩阵。构建并验证了分析性k空间H矩阵用于训练和基准测试。

Result: 在488个增强样本上训练，在46个留出样本上评估。SAN达到最高平均SSIM (0.522)、PSNR (22.09 dB)和最低NMSE (0.233)。配对t检验和Wilcoxon符号秩检验证实SAN在PSNR、NMSE和Pearson相关性上显著优于LISTA (p < 1e-8)，在所有保真度指标上优于ISTA和SBTV。通过绕过H矩阵，SAN将重建时间减少至少一个数量级。

Conclusion: SAN成功实现了无需系统矩阵的实时PAT重建，在图像质量和计算效率上均显著优于现有方法，为临床实时光声成像提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Photoacoustic tomography (PAT) combines the optical absorption contrast of biological tissue with the spatial resolution of ultrasound, yet recovering the initial pressure distribution from sparse-view sensor measurements remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Iterative compressive-sensing solvers and unrolled deep networks both retain a dependence on the system matrix at inference, which leaves real-time clinical reconstruction computationally expensive. This paper proposes the Sensor Attention Network (SAN), a Transformer-based architecture that treats the full time series of each sensor as a token and maps raw measurements directly to the reconstructed image without invoking the system matrix at inference. For training and benchmarking, an analytical k-space H-matrix is constructed and validated against the k-Wave pseudo-spectral solver under matched geometry, achieving a mean per-sensor Pearson correlation of 0.919 +/- 0.049, with k-space apodization and Gaussian temporal damping acting synergistically to reduce the energy-normalized mismatch by 49%. Trained with a vessel-weighted loss on 488 augmented samples and evaluated on 46 held-out samples against ISTA, split-Bregman total variation (SBTV), and learned ISTA (LISTA), SAN attains the highest mean SSIM (0.522) and PSNR (22.09 dB) and the lowest NMSE (0.233). Paired t-tests and Wilcoxon signed-rank tests confirm the superiority of SAN over LISTA on PSNR, NMSE, and Pearson correlation at p < 1e-8, and over ISTA and SBTV on all fidelity metrics. By bypassing the H-matrix at inference, SAN reduces reconstruction time by at least an order of magnitude, supporting real-time PAT reconstruction.

</details>


### [105] [How Small Can You Go? A Controlled Study of LoRA Rank, Target Modules, and Quantization Trade-offs for Text-to-SQL on a 60M-Parameter Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25583)
*Mahendra Singh Rathor,Anagheem Azzam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在60M参数的T5-small模型上系统研究了PEFT与量化技术的效率-精度权衡。LoRA(r=16)以不到1%的可训练参数恢复全量微调82%的性能，QLoRA(INT8/NF4)在0.6GB显存下实现相当精度，为资源受限场景提供了明确的技术选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT与量化研究多聚焦于十亿参数级模型，设计空间探索成本高昂，缺乏针对小规模模型（如60M参数）的系统性效率-精度权衡研究。本文旨在可控环境下量化分析各效率调节手段（LoRA秩、适应模块选择、数值精度）对任务精度的实际影响，将模型适应问题重构为多目标约束优化而非单纯追求精度。

Method: 在完全可复现的60M参数编码器-解码器模型(T5-small)与单表文本转SQL基准(WikiSQL)上开展控制变量研究：系统评估LoRA秩r∈{2,4,8,16,32}、适应模块集合及数值精度三个维度；同步测量任务精度与系统级指标（可训练参数量、峰值训练显存、推理延迟、吞吐量）；对比QLoRA(INT8/NF4量化)方案。

Result: LoRA(r=16)达到59.6%精确匹配率（全量微调为71.2%），仅训练<1%参数并节省31%峰值显存；秩超过16无精度增益。QLoRA(INT8/NF4)以52.8%/53.2%精度将显存需求降至0.60GB。实验揭示了精度、显存、参数量之间的明确权衡关系。

Conclusion: 研究证明小规模模型上PEFT与量化可实现高效的精度-资源权衡：LoRA(r=16)是最优秩选择，QLoRA在极端内存受限场景具有显著优势。所有代码、配置与日志已开源，为实际部署提供了可复现的技术决策框架，强调了多目标系统级评估的重要性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and low-bit quantization are now standard tools for adapting language models under tight compute budgets, yet their interaction is most often studied on billion-parameter models where the design space is expensive to explore. We ask a complementary question: on a specific, fully reproducible 60M-parameter encoder-decoder model (T5-small) and a single-table text-to-SQL benchmark (WikiSQL), how much task accuracy does each efficiency knob actually cost? We run a controlled, single-variable study over (i) LoRA rank r in {2, 4, 8, 16, 32}, (ii) the set of adapted modules, and (iii) numerical precision. We report task accuracy alongside system-level metrics including trainable parameters, peak training memory, inference latency, and throughput, and frame adaptation as a constrained trade-off rather than an accuracy-only objective. Our results show that LoRA with r=16 recovers within 11.6 percentage points of full fine-tuning accuracy (59.6% vs. 71.2% exact-match) while training fewer than 1% of parameters and consuming 31% less peak GPU memory. Within this setting, rank beyond r=16 yields no measurable accuracy gain. QLoRA with INT8 and NF4 quantization achieves comparable accuracy (52.8% and 53.2%) at dramatically lower memory cost (0.60 GB each), demonstrating a compelling trade-off for memory-constrained deployments. All code, configurations, and logs are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [106] [A Density-Matrix Framework for Electronic-Structure Analysis of Functional-Group and Salt Effects in Lithium-Metal Electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25597)
*Mingkang Liu,Huize Yu,Yanbin Gao,Nan Yao,Xiang Chen,Lei Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发了基于密度矩阵的AI平台EMolStudio，用于高效预测锂金属电解质的电子结构，揭示了官能团和盐阴离子对前线轨道、静电势和键级的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂金属电解质的反应性源于分子官能团、Li⁺溶剂化和盐阴离子参与的复杂相互作用，这种作用通过电子密度重分布实现。量子化学计算能准确解析电子结构，但在多维设计空间中计算成本高昂；现有机器学习模型难以覆盖化学多样性溶剂化壳层或电解质相关读出量。

Method: 提出以密度矩阵为中心的AI平台EMolStudio，集成分子官能化、显式Li⁺第一壳层组装、带幂等性投影的密度矩阵预测，以及前线轨道、静电势、Li⁺-给体键级和电子定域化等读出分析。平台应用于163,655个官能化分子和22,500个显式Li⁺第一壳层簇，涵盖四种锂盐体系。

Result: 在分子尺度上，官能化使CO₂Me、CN、F/CF₃和磺酰基在前线能级、静电势和Li⁺-给体接触上呈现化学差异，符合π*受体、诱导和极化贡献机制，高官能化程度下呈现亚线性累积效应。在显式溶剂化壳层中，阴离子身份重塑前线轨道定域化：LiTDI使HOMO在全库中锚定于阴离子，而LiDFOB使阴离子承载HOMO且LUMO承载强烈依赖于官能团。

Conclusion: EMolStudio将官能团和盐选择转化为与锂键形成、去溶剂化和界面反应相关的电子结构假设，为锂金属电解质设计提供高效的理论平台。

Abstract: The reactivity of lithium-metal electrolytes arises from the interplay of molecular functional groups, Li$^+$ solvation, and salt-anion participation. This interplay operates through the redistribution of electron density across donor, anion, and cation centers, which is most directly read out from the electronic structure resolved in space. Quantum-chemical calculations deliver such readouts faithfully, yet become computationally demanding across this multidimensional design space, and machine-learning electronic-structure models seldom cover chemically diverse solvation shells or electrolyte-relevant readouts. Here, we present a density-matrix-centered AI platform (EMolStudio) for electronic-structure prediction and analysis. Its workflow integrates molecular functionalization, explicit Li$^+$ first-shell assembly, density-matrix prediction with idempotency projection, and readouts of frontier orbitals, electrostatic potential, Li$^+$-donor bond order, and electron localization. We apply EMolStudio to 163,655 functionalized molecules and 22,500 explicit Li$^+$ first-shell clusters across four lithium salts. We find that 1) at the molecular scale, functionalization distinguishes CO$_2$Me, CN, F/CF$_3$, and sulfonyl groups by chemically distinct changes in frontier levels, electrostatic potential, and Li$^+$-donor contact, consistent with $π^*$-acceptor, inductive, and polarization contributions, with sublinear accumulation at higher degrees of functionalization; 2) in explicit solvation shells, anion identity reshapes frontier-orbital localization: LiTDI anchors the HOMO on the anion across the entire library, whereas LiDFOB pairs an anion-hosted HOMO with strongly functional-group-dependent LUMO hosting. EMolStudio thereby translates functional-group and salt choices into electronic-structure hypotheses relevant to lithium-bond formation, desolvation, and interphase reactions.

</details>


### [107] [Computational Extraction of Legal Causes via al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim: A Set-Theoretic Formalization for Closed Fiqh Chapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25605)
*Elnaser Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用集合论形式化经典伊斯兰法学方法（al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim）的框架，并开发了从法学裁决真值表中提取最小操作规则的算法，能够计算最小结构生成器并消除逻辑冗余属性，从而为后续法学评估提供候选原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了形式化伊斯兰法学中经典的“al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim”（审查与划分）方法，实现法律原因（'ilal）提取的精确化和计算化，从而在法学封闭章节中系统地识别和消除冗余属性。

Method: 采用集合论形式化方法，基于法学裁决的完整真值表，开发计算算法以提取最小操作规则，计算裁决的最小结构生成器，并消除所有逻辑冗余属性。该方法依赖于有限的学派相对概念词汇表和章节的完整裁决表。

Result: 给定封闭章节的完整真值表，算法能够计算裁决的最小结构生成器，消除所有逻辑冗余属性，生成可作为后续法学评估候选原因的合结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功实现了经典法学方法的计算化形式化，为法律原因的提取提供了系统性工具，但其应用受到概念词汇表有限性和章节裁决表完整性的条件限制。

Abstract: This paper presents a set-theoretic formalization of the classical usuli method of al-Sabr wa al-Taqsim (Examination and Division) for extracting legal causes ('ilal) within closed chapters of jurisprudence. A computational algorithm is introduced that extracts minimal operational rules from a truth table of juristic verdicts. The principal result is that, given a complete truth table for a closed chapter, the algorithm computes the minimal structural generators of the ruling and eliminates all logically redundant attributes. The resulting structures constitute admissible candidate causes for subsequent juristic evaluation. The framework is conditional upon the availability of a finite school-relative concept vocabulary and a complete ruling table for the chapter under investigation.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Sensor Alignment for Weather Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25612)
*Samsad Alam,Devyani Lambhate,Aditya Mohan,Vishal Kumar,Vaibhav Katewa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDAM方法和Unified-weather-edit方法，用于解决自动驾驶恶劣天气仿真中的天气特征对齐问题，发现对齐后的仿真数据能更准确评估3D检测模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知任务需要在恶劣天气条件下可靠工作，但缺乏真实恶劣天气数据集。天气仿真是可行的替代方案，但现有仿真需要确保与真实天气数据具有相同的天气特征，包括严重程度和粒子位置，且需要跨不同传感器保持一致。

Method: 提出Reference Dataset Alignment Method (ReDAM)用于雾天强度对齐；提出Unified-weather-edit方法（基于Weather-edit）用于雨雪天气中的粒子位置对齐。通过统计测试和几何测试分别验证两种对齐方法的有效性。

Result: 非对齐版本的3D检测模型相比对齐版本表现出过度乐观的性能估计；通过在对齐的多传感器仿真数据上微调现有的传感器融合模型，证明了该方法在实现3D目标检测任务鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 对齐后的多传感器天气仿真能够更准确地反映真实天气特征，为自动驾驶系统在恶劣天气条件下的鲁棒性评估和改进提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Perception tasks for autonomous vehicles need to work satisfactorily in adverse weather conditions. Due to lack of real-world weather datasets, weather simulations are a promising alternative. To ensure simulations closely mirror real-world weather data, it's crucial that they represent the same weather characteristics, including severity and particle positioning, across different sensors. To achieve this, we propose the Reference Dataset Alignment Method (ReDAM) for weather intensity alignment in fog and Unified-weather-edit (inspired by Weather-edit[1]) for particle positioning alignment in rain and snow. We validate both alignment methods using statistical and geometrical tests, respectively. We find that 3D detection models for non-aligned versions tend to be overly optimistic as compared to aligned versions. We also show the aligned-multi-sensor simulation's effectiveness for achieving robustness for 3D object detection task by finetuning existing sensor fusion models on it.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Epistemia: Epistemic Schizologia and Large Language Models as Techno-Semiotic Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25620)
*Federico Cabitza,Gianluca Colombo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视LLM引发的"Epistemia"现象，提出"epistemic schizologia"概念来描述符号表达与社会解释实践之间的技术性分裂，主张应关注完整的社会技术实践而非孤立的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者接受Quattrociocchi等人关于LLM流利输出可能导致"Epistemia"（在没有正当认识实践的情况下产生知识拥有感）的诊断，但挑战其解释框架——该框架将具身的社会化人类认知者与孤立的生成模型进行比较，从而将认识合法性定位于自主代理人的内部能力。

Method: 采用Carlo Sini关于实践、书写、符号和技术的哲学，将LLM理解为"技术-符号机器"（techno-semiotic machine），它通过从人类书写的沉积档案中产生合理的语言配置来自动化书面符号过程的一个阶段。

Result: 提出"epistemic schizologia"（认识分裂症）概念：符号作为语言表达与符号作为社会性解释、证据、批评、验证和责任回路中的时刻之间的社会技术性分裂。这种分裂由"eikotic closure"（通过将合理的延续以认识结果的最终性呈现）、算法权威和认识自我误认所强化。

Conclusion: 相关分析单位不应是孤立的模型，而是完整的实践——生成的文本在其中被提示、解释、验证、争议、使用并产生后果。这一重构既保持了语言生产与负责任理解之间的区别，又为以可检查的谱系、可争议性、分布式责任、认识代理和混合人-AI实践评估为核心的设计方案奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quattrociocchi and colleagues warn that the fluent outputs of large language models may allow linguistic plausibility to substitute for epistemic evaluation, producing the condition they call *Epistemia*: the experience of possessing knowledge without undertaking the practices through which judgment would ordinarily be warranted. This article accepts that diagnosis but challenges its explanatory framework, which compares an embodied, socially situated human knower with an isolated generative model thereby locating epistemic legitimacy in capacities internal to autonomous agents. Drawing on Carlo Sini's philosophy of practices, writing, signs, and technics, we propose instead to understand a large language model (LLM) as a *techno-semiotic machine* that automates a phase of written semiosis by producing plausible linguistic configurations from the sedimented archive of human writing. From this perspective, *Epistemia* is one consequence of a broader phenomenon that we call *epistemic schizologia*: the socio-technical cleavage between signs as linguistically accomplished expressions and signs as moments within socially embedded circuits of interpretation, evidence, criticism, verification, and responsibility. This cleavage is reinforced by *eikotic closure*, through which a plausible continuation is presented with the finality of an epistemic result, and by algorithmic authority and epistemic self-misrecognition. The relevant unit is therefore not the model alone but the complete practice in which generated inscriptions are prompted, interpreted, verified, contested, used, and made consequential. This reframing preserves the distinction between linguistic production and responsible understanding while grounding a design programme centred on inspectable genealogy, contestability, distributed responsibility, epistemic agency, and the evaluation of hybrid human--AIpractices.

</details>


### [110] [Quotient Dynamics, Effective Curvature, and Implicit Bias in Positive Quadratic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25624)
*Pengcheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了正二次网络的商结构如何影响训练动力学、曲率、恢复和插值偏差，证明了因子梯度流与商黎曼梯度流的精确对应关系，并建立了恢复保证和插值解的选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正二次网络具有低秩表示f_U(x)=x^⊤UU^⊤x，其中U只能通过右正交乘法识别，这种商结构的几何性质对理解训练动力学、恢复保证和插值偏差至关重要，但现有研究缺乏对此结构的系统分析。

Method: 将满列秩层上的ℝ^{d×r}_*/O(r)与秩r的PSD流形建立同构关系，研究光滑目标函数L(U)=ℓ(UU^⊤)的梯度动力学性质。分析二次回归中的有效Hessian，在高斯秩一测量下推导种群曲率并建立谱初始化器。通过联合谱坐标分析欠定交换情形下的熵镜像流。

Result: 证明了因子梯度流精确投影到商黎曼梯度流，有限步梯度下降对预测器诱导精确的同余递归。在高斯秩一测量下建立了梯度流的局部指数收敛和小步下降的线性收敛。证明了严格正初始化收敛到插值集上的Bregman投影，各向同性初始化下预测器趋近最小迹解集，解决了非唯一性问题。

Conclusion: 商结构为理解正二次网络的训练动力学提供了统一框架，揭示了梯度下降算法的内在几何性质。恢复保证虽然明确但较为保守，插值解的选择通过加权熵机制实现，有限步下降与连续时间Bregman投影相差O(η)，数值实验验证了所有理论预测。

Abstract: Positive quadratic networks admit the low-rank representation f_U(x)=x^top UU^top x, where Uinmathbb{R}^{dtimes r} is identifiable only up to right orthogonal multiplication, representing a rank-r PSD matrix Q=UU^top. We study how this quotient structure governs training dynamics, curvature, recovery, and interpolation bias. On the full-column-rank stratum, we identify mathbb{R}^{dtimes r}_*/O(r) with the rank-r PSD manifold. For smooth objectives L(U)=ell(UU^top), the Euclidean factor gradient is horizontal. Thus, factor gradient flow projects exactly to quotient Riemannian gradient flow, while finite-step gradient descent induces an exact congruence recursion for the predictor. For quadratic regression, we derive the effective Hessian at interpolators as the empirical measurement Gram form restricted to the tangent space relative to the quotient metric. Under Gaussian rank-one measurements, we compute population curvature, prove uniform deviation bounds for the empirical normal operator, construct a spectral initializer, and establish local exponential convergence for gradient flow and linear convergence for small-step descent. Recovery guarantees are explicit but conservative due to reliance on full-space second-moment control. In underdetermined commuting regimes, factor gradient flow becomes an exact entropy mirror flow in joint spectral coordinates. Strictly positive initializations converge to Bregman projections onto the interpolation set. With isotropic initialization q(0)=varepsilon^2mathbf{1}, predictors approach the minimum-trace solution set as varepsilondownarrow0, resolving nonuniqueness via weighted entropy within the invariant joint spectral algebra. Finite-step descent selects interpolants differing from continuous-time Bregman projections by O(eta). Numerical experiments verify these quotient identities, curvature predictions, recovery behaviors, and selection laws.

</details>


### [111] [Joint Text-Audio Alignment for EEG-to-Text Decoding in Chinese Speech Production and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25626)
*Tian Zheng,Xurong Xie,Xinxin Zhu,Xiaolan Peng,Feng Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EEGAlign框架，首次实现了从非侵入式脑电信号解码大规模中文句子，在朗读任务中达到82.37%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非侵入式EEG语音解码方法难以处理中文句子解码中的高维输出空间、严重的受试者间差异和低信噪比问题。单一监督轴（文本语义或音频声学特征）无法同时满足大规模中文解码所需的句子级区分性和细粒度时序分辨率需求。

Method: 提出EEGAlign框架，通过对比学习联合对齐EEG与两个监督轴：使用BGE-M3文本嵌入进行文本对齐，使用wav2vec 2.0语音特征进行音频对齐，随后进行CTC字符序列解码。该方法实现了参数高效的联合对齐学习。

Result: 在ChineseEEG-2数据集上，EEGAlign在闭集句子分类任务中取得了最先进的性能：朗读EEG任务中Top-1准确率达到82.37%，被动听力EEG任务中达到41.43%（101个候选句子）。消融研究表明两个对齐轴高度互补，联合使用效果优于单一轴。

Conclusion: 这是首个从朗读过程中的非侵入式EEG解码大规模中文句子的研究，在相对大规模闭集候选句子设置下实现了强大的分类性能，证明了多轴联合对齐方法在中文脑电语音解码中的有效性。

Abstract: Decoding speech information directly from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) into text provides a potential non-invasive neural communication pathway for individuals with severe speech and motor impairments. Compared with invasive approaches such as electrocorticography, EEG is safer and more widely deployable, yet substantially more challenging to decode.This challenge is exacerbated for Chinese sentence decoding, which must handle a high-dimensional output space with thousands of characters, severe inter-subject variability, and low signal-to-noise ratios for text alignment.Existing methods commit to a single supervisory axis---either text semantics or audio acoustic features---yet neither can simultaneously satisfy the demands of sentence-level discriminability and fine-grained temporal resolution required for large-vocabulary Chinese decoding. We introduce EEGAlign, a novel parameter-efficient framework that jointly aligns EEG with two axes---text alignment with BGE-M3 text embeddings and audio alignment with wav2vec~2.0 speech features via contrastive learning followed by CTC character-sequence decoding. On ChineseEEG-2 data, EEGAlign yields state-of-the-art closed-set sentence classification performance, reaching up to 82.37% Top-1 accuracy on Reading Aloud EEG and 41.43% on Passive Listening EEG out of 101 candidates. Ablation studies show that the two alignment axes are highly complementary: combining them yields consistently better performance than either alone. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study on decoding large-vocabulary Chinese sentences from non-invasive EEG during overt speech production, and achieving strong classification performance with relatively large closed-set candidate-sentence setting.

</details>


### [112] [AIriskEval-edu Demo: Auditing of Pedagogical Risks in Educational Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25634)
*Javier Irigoyen,Roberto Daza,Francisco Jurado,Julian Fierrez,Ruben Tolosana,Alvaro Ortigosa,Miguel Lopez-Duran,Aythami Morales*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AIriskEval-edu Demo平台，用于审核教学解释的 pedagogical 质量，并提供可解释的审核结果。平台集成了GPT-5.5和本地部署的Llama 3.1 8B评估器，后者在大多数指标上表现更优，为教育机构提供了实用的内容审核解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学解释的质量直接影响学习效果，但人工审核耗时费力。现有AI审核工具存在可解释性不足、数据隐私风险等问题。因此需要开发一个能够自动、准确、可解释地审核教学解释质量的平台，帮助教育机构及时发现和纠正教学风险。

Method: 构建了AIriskEval-edu Demo平台，采用双评估器架构：通过外部API集成GPT-5.5，以及本地部署的Llama 3.1 8B评估器。制定了覆盖五个维度的pedagogical风险评分标准：事实准确性、深度与完整性、聚焦与相关性、学生水平适当性、意识形态偏见。对本地评估器在AIriskEval-edu数据集上进行微调。平台支持AI模式（评估六个模拟教师行为）和人工模式（实时审核用户撰写的解释）。

Result: 对于每个评估维度，平台输出二元决策和置信度分数。检测到的风险包含自然语言理由和（除深度与完整性维度外）局部证据片段。本地评估器在大多数报告指标上优于GPT-5.5。平台成功实现了对教学解释的自动审核，并能提供可解释的审计结果。

Conclusion: AIriskEval-edu Demo平台为教育机构提供了一个实用的工具，能够在保持审核内容在自有基础设施内的前提下，自动、可解释地审核教学解释质量，有效弥补了现有审核工具的不足，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present AIriskEval-edu Demo, a platform that audits the pedagogical quality of instructional explanations and provides explainable audit results. The platform evaluates an explanation against a rubric covering five dimensions of pedagogical risk: factual accuracy, depth and completeness, focus and relevance, student-level appropriateness, and ideological bias. For each dimension, it returns a binary decision and a confidence score. Detected risks also include a natural-language rationale and, except for Depth and Completeness, a localized evidence span. The platform integrates GPT-5.5 through an external API and a self-hosted Llama 3.1 8B evaluator that runs on consumer-grade GPUs. The local evaluator is fine-tuned on AIriskEval-edu, a dataset of K-12 instructional explanations with risk and explainability annotations. The platform operates in two modes: in AI mode, both evaluators assess stored explanations generated under six simulated teacher profiles, each representing a distinct pedagogical behavior and potential risk; in human mode, the local evaluator audits user-written explanations in real time. The local evaluator outperforms GPT-5.5 on most reported metrics, offering educational institutions a practical way to keep audited content within their own infrastructure.

</details>


### [113] [Engine-Equal, Human-Unequal: A Reproducible Outcome Skew in Engine-Assessed Equal Chess Positions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25655)
*Jesung Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，即使在国际象棋引擎评估为均势的开局位置中，人类对局结果仍存在系统性偏差，这种偏差具有跨时间、玩家群体和等级分的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在强引擎评估为均势的开局位置中，人类对局结果是否真正平衡，以及是否存在位置本身固有的结果偏差。

Method: 收集Lichess平台2025年10月数据（1,661个位置，1610万次对局），使用Stockfish 18评估筛选均势位置（评估值在零点附近±10厘兵分内），计算每个位置的结果偏差（实际结果与等级分预测结果的差距），通过三种重新划分（不同玩家账户组、时间、等级分段）和一个样本外月份验证偏差的可复制性。

Result: 主要发现包括：1）位置偏差具有稳定性，主要验证中复制斜率为0.69（95%置信区间[0.65, 0.74]），在最流行、测量最准确的位置上高达0.94；2）典型偏差较小（中位数约1.8%白方得分率，约2个百分点）；3）偏差在各种更严格的评估标准、搜索深度、校准方法和流行度阈值下都存在；4）在快棋和超快棋中分别复制；5）处于劣势的一方思考时间更长。

Conclusion: 即使引擎评估最确信的均势位置，引擎评估也不是人类对局结果的充分统计量；存在位置固有的结果偏差，这些偏差虽然是观测性结果，但具有稳健的可复制性，因果问题留待预注册的随机对照研究解决。

Abstract: Among chess opening positions that a strong engine judges essentially equal (Stockfish 18 evaluation within 10 centipawns of zero, depth-stable) and that humans actually reach on Lichess (October 2025; 1,661 positions, 16.1M occurrences), human results are not balanced. Positions carry outcome skews, each the gap between its games' actual results and what the players' ratings predict, whose directions are stable properties of the naturally-reached position: some positions favour White, others Black. These skews reproduce across three re-partitions -- disjoint player-account sets (primary), time, and disjoint rating bands -- and on an out-of-sample month eight months later. On the primary split, each position's skew is measured once in each account group, and the replication slope asks how well one measurement predicts the other after removing rating and opening-family effects: one means undiminished carry-over; zero, no linear relation. We find 0.69 (family-clustered 95% CI [0.65, 0.74]), rising to 0.94 on the most-popular, best-measured positions. The slope's value depends on the position mix. Existence is the invariant claim: it survives every tighter evaluation band, search depth, calibration, and popularity cutoff we test, and replicates within blitz and rapid separately. The typical skew is small (median $|δ| \approx 0.018$, about two percentage points of White score), yet it reproduces, position by position, across disjoint accounts. At these positions the disfavoured side also thinks longer. Even where the evaluation is most confident, it is not a sufficient statistic for human outcomes. The result is observational, and the causal question is left to a pre-registered randomised companion study.

</details>


### [114] [OrchBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Plans in Isolation via Deterministic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25656)
*Zhenzhen Ren,Jiyan He,Xinpeng Zhang,Zhenxing Qian,Ke Han,Shuxin Zheng,GuoBiao Li,Xiaoqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OrchBench，一个基于仿真的基准测试，用于独立评估多智能体系统的编排计划，避免了传统端到端评估的成本高昂和因素混淆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂任务通常分解为可并行化但相互依赖的子任务，编排质量对多智能体系统性能至关重要。现有评估方法依赖端到端执行，存在两个核心问题：（1）混淆了编排计划质量与工作者能力、工具可靠性及环境噪声等因素；（2）随着工作流规模增长，时间和token成本急剧上升，使得系统评估成本昂贵。

Method: 提出OrchBench仿真基准测试框架。从现实任务构建有向无环图（DAG）编码任务依赖关系，控制图规模和并行度。给定DAG、每智能体上下文限制和智能体预算，被评估的规划器将子任务分配给智能体并指定跨智能体信息传输及其保留率。使用确定性模拟器评估计划，无需调用工作者智能体，返回可解释的结果质量、完工时间和token成本度量。

Result: OrchBench产生的模拟分数与Claude Code执行的质量分数高度相关，Pearson相关系数r=0.816，同时仅需1.3%的token和10.3%的运行时间。跨多种规划器和工作流规模的实验发现：保留任务关键信息比简单增加智能体数量更重要；随着协调失败累积，并行化的收益递减。

Conclusion: OrchBench作为高效、可解释的基准测试，能够有效比较和诊断多智能体编排计划，为多智能体系统编排质量评估提供了低成本、高效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Complex tasks often decompose into parallelizable yet interdependent subtasks, making orchestration critical to the performance of multi-agent systems (MAS). Existing evaluations typically rely on end-to-end execution, which conflates orchestration-plan quality with worker capabilities, tool reliability, and environmental noise. Moreover, the time and token costs of real execution grow rapidly with workflow scale, making systematic evaluation expensive. We present OrchBench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating multi-agent orchestration plans in isolation. Starting from real-world tasks, OrchBench constructs directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that encode task dependencies, with controlled sizes and degrees of parallelism. Given a DAG, a per-agent context limit, and an agent budget, the evaluated planner assigns subtasks to agents and specifies cross-agent information transfers and their retention ratios. A deterministic simulator evaluates the resulting plan without invoking worker agents and returns interpretable measures of result quality, makespan, and token cost. The simulated scores produced by OrchBench correlate strongly with quality scores from Claude Code executions, achieving a Pearson correlation of \(r=0.816\), while requiring only \(1.3\%\) of the tokens and \(10.3\%\) of the wall-clock time. Across diverse planners and workflow scales, we find that preserving task-critical information is more important than simply increasing the number of agents, and the benefits of parallelism diminish as coordination failures accumulate. These results establish OrchBench as an efficient and interpretable benchmark for comparing and diagnosing multi-agent orchestration plans.

</details>


### [115] [CoRT: Counterfactual Replay for Token-Level Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25659)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Wen Wang,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CoRT方法，通过反事实回放为基于评分标准的GRPO提供token级信用分配，无需辅助评分模型即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的强化学习（如GRPO）将结构化判断压缩为响应级奖励，并均匀分配给所有token，缺乏响应内部的信用分配机制，无法区分不同评分标准对应的不同文本片段。

Method: 提出CoRT方法，使用反事实回放技术：对同一采样响应用原始评分条件提示和匹配的无条件提示分别评分，将token级对数似然对比作为对评分条件依赖度的代理，生成有界的响应归一化权重来重新分配GRPO优势。

Result: 在多数比较中优于响应级GRPO，平均提升4.4个百分点；与需要学习的token级信用基线方法保持竞争力，同时避免了单独的相关性学习阶段。

Conclusion: 策略内部的反事实似然对比为响应内信用分配提供了有效的训练信号，同时保持了GRPO的简洁性和稳定性。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning enriches language model training by evaluating model outputs against explicit criteria. Yet in GRPO-style pipelines, these structured judgments are reduced to a scalar response-level reward and converted into a response-level advantage, which is broadcast uniformly to all generated tokens. This leaves no explicit mechanism for allocating credit within a response, even when different criteria are grounded in different spans, formatting decisions, or semantic choices. We propose CoRT, a token-level credit weighting method for rubric-conditioned GRPO. Instead of training an auxiliary token scoring model, CoRT uses counterfactual replay to rescore the same sampled response under the original rubric-conditioned prompt and a matched criteria-free prompt. The resulting tokenwise log-likelihood contrasts serve as a proxy for dependence on the rubric context. CoRT maps these contrasts to bounded, response-normalized weights and uses them to redistribute the signed GRPO advantage across tokens, without introducing an auxiliary scorer or changing the response-level reward. Experiments across instruction-tuned models and reward granularities show that CoRT improves over matched response-level GRPO in the vast majority of comparisons, with an average gain of 4.4 percentage points. The method remains competitive with learned token-level credit baselines while avoiding a separate relevance-learning stage. These results suggest that policy-internal counterfactual likelihood contrasts provide an effective training signal for within-response credit allocation while retaining the simplicity and stability of GRPO.

</details>


### [116] [Localized Adaptation Reveals Distinct Learning Signatures in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25663)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统分析了LoRA适配层位置对模型学习行为的影响，发现不同学习目标具有独特的"适配几何"特性，为控制模型的学习、泛化和稳定性提供了关键设计依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型的适配通常分布在模型深度上，即使预期的变化范围很窄也是如此。研究者希望探究适配位置如何影响模型的学习内容、泛化能力以及学习的选择性应用，从而实现对模型行为的精确控制。

Method: 构建了一个包含五个目标（词汇绑定、事实关联、行为策略学习、因果映射和程序推理）的控制基准，定义了每个目标的"适配几何"——即在全栈、早期层、中期层或晚期层LoRA适配下的习得、迁移和边界性特征图谱。通过参数匹配对照和跨五个模型家族的验证来确保结论的可靠性。

Result: 不同目标展现出独特的适配几何：词汇绑定倾向于早期层适配以实现习得和边界性，但需要更广泛的更新来实现迁移；事实关联在局部适配器中倾向于后期层；行为学习将晚期层的动作习得与中期层的策略门控分离；因果和程序迁移最受益于中期层或全栈适配。这些模式在参数匹配对照下基本保持稳定，且大多数方向性对比在五个模型家族中得到复现。

Conclusion: 适配层位置是控制模型学习内容、泛化能力和保持不变特性的关键设计变量，不同学习目标需要针对性地选择适配位置以实现最优的学习效果和控制精度。

Abstract: Transformer adaptation is typically distributed across model depth, even when the intended change is narrow. We investigate how adaptation site shapes what a model learns, how well that learning generalizes, and how selectively it is applied. We introduce a controlled benchmark spanning five objectives (lexical binding, factual association, behavioral policy learning, causal mapping, and procedural reasoning) and define each objective's "adaptation geometry" as its profile of acquisition, transfer, and boundedness under full-stack and early-, middle-, or late-layer LoRA. The objectives exhibit distinct geometries. Lexical binding favors early-layer adaptation for acquisition and boundedness but requires broader updates for transfer; factual association favors later layers among localized adapters; behavioral learning separates late-layer action acquisition from middle-layer policy gating; and causal and procedural transfer benefit most from middle- or full-stack adaptation. These patterns largely persist under parameter-matched controls, and most corresponding directional contrasts replicate across five model families. These findings establish adaptation site as a key design variable for controlling what models learn, generalize, and leave unchanged.

</details>


### [117] [OmniDelta: Skill-Driven Budget Allocation for Token Compression in OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25669)
*Haoyang Huang,Wenjie Huang,Tianqi Xu,Hongyaoxing Gu,Kang Tan,Yikai Fu,Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Baolin Zhang,Jun Zhang,Xinyi Hu,Jun Dai,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingchen Wang,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OmniDelta框架，通过意图感知的跨模态分配和内容感知的模态内分配优化多模态大语言模型的token压缩，在保持准确性的同时显著降低了内存和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全模态大语言模型（OmniLLMs）在处理长音频视频token序列时存在显著的内存和推理成本问题。现有压缩方法主要关注在固定预算下选择重要token，但忽略了预算分配问题。直接使用查询-音频/视频相似度进行跨模态预算分配不可靠，而均匀的模态内预算分配可能遗漏关键证据同时保留冗余内容。

Method: 提出了OmniDelta，一个免训练、技能驱动的框架，结合意图感知的跨模态分配和内容感知的模态内分配。该方法首先构建音频和视频技能池，根据查询需求调整固定的保留token预算，然后利用局部复杂度和时间冗余性在音频片段和视频帧上重新分配模态预算。生成的局部预算可与现有剪枝策略结合，保持总保留token比例的同时改变预算分配位置。

Result: 在四个音频-视频基准测试和两个Qwen2.5-Omni模型上的实验表明，OmniDelta在各个剪枝比例下建立了新的准确性-效率帕累托前沿。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上以25%的token保留率，OmniDelta将GPU内存减少了22.0%，相比全token推理实现了1.64倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: OmniDelta有效解决了全模态大语言模型中音频视频token序列的压缩问题，通过智能的预算分配策略在保持模型性能的同时显著提升了推理效率，为多模态模型的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Emerging Omni-modal Large Language Models (OmniLLMs) enable unified understanding of text, audio, and video, but their long audio-video token sequences introduce substantial memory and inference costs. Existing compression methods mainly focus on selecting important tokens under fixed budgets, leaving the preceding budget-allocation problem underexplored. We show that direct query-to-audio/video similarity is unreliable for inter-modal budget allocation, and that uniform intra-modal budgets can miss key evidence while retaining redundant content. To address these limitations, we propose OmniDelta, a training-free, skill-driven framework that couples intent-aware inter-modal allocation with content-aware intra-modal allocation. OmniDelta first constructs audio and video skill pools to shift the fixed retained-token budget according to query demand, then reallocates modality budgets over audio segments and video frames using local complexity and temporal redundancy. The resulting local budgets can be combined with existing pruning strategies, preserving the total retained-token ratio while changing where the budget is spent. Experiments on four audio-video benchmarks with two Qwen2.5-Omni models show that OmniDelta establishes a new accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier across pruning ratios. At 25% token retention on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniDelta reduces GPU memory by 22.0% and achieves a 1.64x end-to-end speedup over full-token inference.

</details>


### [118] [DecoEvo: Score-Decoupled Co-Evolution of Solver and Rubric-Generator Skills in Text Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25675)
*Jiangwang Chen,Zixin Song,Junlin Liu,Shuaiyu Zhou,Haiyan Wu,Haihan Shi,Chenxi Zhou,Hanqing Li,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DecoEvo框架，通过解耦目标函数协同演化求解器和评分标准生成器，解决了现有文本空间优化方法在开放式任务中的评估瓶颈问题，在五个基准测试上取得2.8-5.0%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本空间优化方法在开放式任务中存在评估瓶颈：当求解器在评分标准所衡量的维度上改进后，未涵盖的维度对优化信号不可见。简单地演化评分标准也不可靠，因为当前求解器分数选择的更新可能使评分标准更容易满足，而非真正进步。

Method: 提出DecoEvo（解耦协同演化）框架，在无黄金评分标准的优化过程中协同演化求解器技能和评分标准生成器技能。求解器使用标准级反馈更新，评分标准生成器通过独立于求解器总分的需求覆盖度和响应区分度的互补审计进行修正。这种分离使生成器更新聚焦于新暴露的求解器弱点。

Result: 在五个基准测试和三个LLM骨干网络的官方评估下，DecoEvo优于所有对比方法。在五个基准测试的平均值上，相对于SkillOpt获得2.8-5.0%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过解耦目标函数协同演化求解器和评分标准生成器，DecoEvo有效解决了开放式任务中的评估瓶颈问题，避免了评分标准退化的风险，实现了更全面的性能提升。

Abstract: Text-space optimization adapts large language models (LLMs) by editing external natural-language artifacts rather than model weights, so the optimized artifacts remain inspectable and the model can be treated as a black box. However, most existing text-space methods keep evaluation fixed. On open-ended tasks, this can become a bottleneck: once the solver improves on the criteria a rubric measures, omitted dimensions remain invisible to the optimization signal. Simply evolving the rubric is also unreliable when updates are selected by the current solver's score, because apparent progress can come from making the rubric easier to satisfy. We introduce DecoEvo (Decoupled Co-Evolution), which co-evolves a solver skill and a rubric-generator skill under decoupled objectives without using gold rubrics during optimization. The solver skill is updated using criterion-level feedback, while the rubric-generator skill is revised through complementary audits of requirement coverage and response discrimination that are independent of aggregate solver score. This separation focuses generator updates on newly exposed solver weaknesses, reducing repeated emphasis on criteria the solver already satisfies. Under each benchmark's official evaluation, DecoEvo outperforms all compared methods across five benchmarks and three LLM backbones, yielding 2.8--5.0\% relative gains over SkillOpt in the five-benchmark average.

</details>


### [119] [Cognivia: A Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Copilot for Evidence-Based Mental Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25681)
*Qi Chen,Siria Xiyueyao Luo,Jian Wang,Yuan Shi,Haocong Rao,Xuejiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cognivia，一个基于认知行为疗法（CBT）的AI治疗师，能够自动识别认知扭曲并生成理性回应，在专家评估中表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知扭曲会加剧负面情绪并导致心理健康障碍，虽然认知行为疗法（CBT）是有效干预手段，但专业治疗师短缺限制了其大规模应用。现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的心理健康应用方法存在领域专业性不足、回应过于迎合、缺乏明确的认知扭曲标注等问题。

Method: 基于权威CBT文本构建框架，结合心理健康问答数据进行增强，在行为科学专家监督下采用多阶段提示和结构化生成策略。在此基础上对轻量级LLM进行微调得到Cognivia。同时提出了首个用于评估LLM生成理性回应的层次化质量评估框架。

Result: Cognivia在词汇指标、基于LLM的评判（两种互补标准）以及10位行为科学专家的人工评估中，在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成方面均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Cognivia证明了基于证据的AI治疗师在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成方面的有效性，为解决专业治疗师短缺问题提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Cognitive distortion amplifies negative emotions and contributes to mental health disorders. Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) is an effective way to address cognitive distortions, but its large-scale application is limited by the shortage of professional therapists. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been explored for mental health applications, existing methods still suffer from limited domain specificity, overly flattering responses, and the absence of well-defined annotations for cognitive distortions. This paper proposes Cognivia, an evidence-based artificial intelligence therapist that integrates automatic cognitive distortion identification and rational response generation. Our framework is built on authoritative CBT texts widely regarded as core paradigms and standard references. It is further augmented with mental health question-answer (Q and A) data, and employs multi-stage prompting and structured generation strategies under the supervision of behavioral science experts. Then we fine-tune a lightweight LLM on this augmented CBT dataset to obtain Cognivia. In addition, we propose the first hierarchical quality evaluation framework for assessing LLM-generated rational responses, developed through collaboration between AI researchers and behavioral science experts. Cognivia is evaluated using lexical metrics, LLM-based Judges with two complementary criteria, and human evaluation by 10 behavioral science experts. It consistently outperforms the baseline methods in cognitive distortion recognition and rational response generation, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/SNOWTEAM2023/Cognivia.

</details>


### [120] [Nudging Sustainable Choices through LLM-Generated Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25726)
*Haya Halimeh,Dietmar Jannach,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了推荐系统解释中可持续性信息的不同行为框架如何影响用户选择。研究发现，仅仅披露可持续性信息无法改变用户选择，而通过特定的行为框架（如描述性社会规范）可以显著增加可持续选择并简化决策过程。研究还展示了如何利用生成式AI大规模生成基于理论的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统在日常生活中扮演着重要角色，为鼓励可持续选择提供了一个有前景的渠道。先前研究表明，解释会影响用户对推荐的感知，并能支持更明智的决策。研究者认为，解释还可以在决策时刻通过突出可持续性信息来充当行为助推器（behavioral nudges），从而影响用户的可持续选择行为。

Method: 1. 利用生成式AI结合助推理论生成包含可持续性信息的推荐解释，并通过人类评估和LLM-as-a-judge进行验证。
2. 开展两项随机研究（N=529），分别涉及低参与度领域（速溶咖啡）和高参与度领域（酒店预订）。
3. 参与者在偏好匹配的推荐中进行选择，这些推荐配有不同框架的解释。
4. 对比分析不同解释框架对用户选择和感知的影响。

Result: 1. 在两个领域中，仅在解释中披露可持续性信息并不会改变用户选择。
2. 对可持续性信息进行特定框架处理或调用描述性社会规范，可以显著增加可持续选择并简化决策过程。
3. 感知与行为存在差异：简单的信息披露可以提升用户对解释的评估，但并不能转化为更多的可持续选择行为。
4. 验证了LLMs可以大规模生成基于理论的解释。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于理论的解释干预措施在社会公益方面的潜力，为自适应解释设计提供了实践指导。研究指出，简单地披露可持续性信息不足以改变行为，需要结合行为框架（如社会规范）才能有效促进可持续选择。同时，研究为未来利用生成式AI进行自适应解释设计提供了新的方向和启示。

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [121] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 概念瓶颈模型仅凭中间变量预测准确度评估有效性是不够的。本文发现在无直接监督时，概念层可能失去物理尺度，但仍能保持下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型通常通过中间变量的预测准确度来评估有效性，但作者质疑这种评估方式是否充分。以超声心动图视频中左心室容积作为概念变量、射血分数作为最终预测任务为例，探讨仅用准确度评估概念层的局限性。

Method: 在公开超声心动图数据集上训练视频Transformer编码器，构建以收缩末期和舒张末期容积为概念层的概念瓶颈模型，从这两个容积解析计算射血分数（无残差路径）。对比两种训练策略：（1）仅使用射血分数损失；（2）同时使用容积（毫升）监督损失。在1276个留出研究上进行评估。

Result: 概念瓶颈模型未增加射血分数误差（6.89 vs 直接回归7.13 MAE）。但无容积监督时，预测容积分布坍缩至0.1毫升（参考分布为35.7和45.7毫升），尽管相关性部分保留。原因是射血分数作为比率，当两个容积被同时缩放时保持不变，损失函数仅能确定概念层至尺度不变。添加绝对单位监督使容积误差从89.8降至25.8毫升，代价是射血分数误差增加0.4。

Conclusion: 仅凭概念准确度无法揭示概念层是否携带正确的物理尺度；概念层可能仅保留相对关系而非绝对尺度。临床模型中的可解释中间变量应针对训练目标的尺度不变性进行验证，而非仅依赖预测准确度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [122] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种将Agent与推测器统一在同一模型中的自推测Agent框架，通过联合强化学习训练，显著提升了工具调用预测准确率，有效隐藏了工具调用等待延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型Agent在执行工具调用时需要等待结果返回，导致大量等待时间。虽然工具调用推测可以通过预测并预执行下一个工具调用来隐藏延迟，但现有的推测器（如单独的草稿模型或缓存轨迹）与实际部署的Agent行为存在偏差，导致推测效果不佳。作者识别了这一推测器-Agent差距问题。

Method: 提出自推测Agent（self-speculating agent），在单一模型中统一Agent和推测器两种角色：Agent模式负责任务求解，推测器模式基于部分轨迹预测下一个工具调用，完全复用前缀KV缓存。为保证性能不下降，设计了联合Agent-推测器强化学习方法：从Agent自身的rollout中推导推测目标，并交替更新Agent和推测器。

Result: 在Agent搜索QA和对话式工具使用任务上，Qwen3-4B的平均下一个工具调用Hit@1从44.1提升至61.2，Qwen3-5-4B从48.9提升至66.3，同时保持了Agent任务成功率。

Conclusion: 目标Agent本身是强有效的下一步调用推测器。将Agent和推测器统一在同一模型中的设计更简单且更有效，通过联合强化学习可以在不损害Agent任务性能的前提下显著提升推测准确率，从而有效降低工具调用延迟。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [123] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模分布式约束优化问题(DCOPs)提出在线学习算法和分解框架，在卫星调度任务中将任务完成率从87%提升至99%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的许多DCOP实例规模过大，无法以单体方式求解，需要新的方法来处理大规模分布式决策问题，特别是在大规模去中心化卫星调度场景中。

Method: 从两个互补方向解决挑战：1) 重新审视DCOPs与势博弈的联系，将现代在线学习算法应用于DCOPs的均衡求解；2) 提出新的分解框架，将DCOP分离为任务分配的高层元DCOP和调度的独立局部优化问题，并开发迭代定价方法连接两个层次。

Result: 在线学习算法与代表性不完全DCOP算法具有竞争力；结合在线学习方法和迭代定价框架后，在真实去中心化卫星调度问题实例上实现近最优性能，完成了超过99%的观测请求，相比最先进基准方法的87%有显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线学习算法和分解框架的结合为大规模DCOPs提供了有效的解决方案，能够显著提升大规模去中心化卫星调度等实际应用的性能。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [124] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HiSkill分层技能图框架，通过有向图组织交互轨迹并捕获多种技能关系，在长视野交互任务中优于现有基线并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能转换方法通常生成扁平的高级文本技能集合，独立存储和检索，导致技能关系未被充分利用，且高级技能与可执行动作之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出HiSkill分层技能图框架，将交互轨迹组织成包含技能节点、AtomicOp节点和类型化边的有向图。图中连接可重用的高级技能与可执行动作模板，同时捕获分解、时序转换、兼容性、支持和恢复等关系。推理时检索任务相关子图，通过符号任务状态、活动技能和检索子图引导LLM代理迭代执行技能切换、AtomicOp选择和动作实例化。

Result: 在三个交互环境上的实验表明，HiSkill优于最先进的基线方法，同时降低了推理token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过分层技能图有效桥接了高级技能与可执行动作实例化，验证了该方法在长视野交互任务中的有效性和效率。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges.
